c^{n,\ell m}_{j-1}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{n,\ell m}_{i}}=-\sum_{u=j}^{i}\Delta^{n}_{u}c^{\ell m}
, when
j-1<i
, and using the usual convention that
\sum_{j=n+1}^{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{0}=0
, we have

\displaystyle R+\widetilde{V}^{n,\ell m}_{3}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\left[c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-c_{i}^{n,\ell m}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\left[c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-c_{i}^{n,\ell m}\right]

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=2}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{j-2}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\sum_{u=i+1}^{j-1}\Delta^{n}_{u}c^{\ell m}

\displaystyle\quad-\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\sum_{u=j}^{i}\Delta^{n}_{u}c^{\ell m}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{u=1}^{n}\Delta_{u}^{n}c^{\ell m}\left[\sum_{j=u+1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\sum_{j=1}^{u}\sum_{i=u}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=:\int_{0}^{T}H(n)_{t}dc^{\ell m}_{t},

(A.39)

where,
for
t\in\left((u-1)\Delta_{n},u\Delta_{n}\right)
,

H(n)_{t}:=\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left[\sum_{j=u+1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\sum_{j=1}^{u}\sum_{i=u}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right].

(A.40)

Note
H(n)_{t}
is adapted to
\mathcal{F}_{t}
. We first consider the first term in (
A.40
).
It can be shown (see Appendix
B.2
) that

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{u+1}}^{T}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds=o_{p}(1).

(A.41)

Next, we show that
\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{t_{u+1}}^{T}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds
converges to
\theta\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\int_{0}^{\infty}L(v)dv
. Indeed, since

\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{u}}^{t_{u+1}}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}|K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})|d\varpi ds\leq C\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{u}}^{t_{u+1}}\int_{\frac{s-t_{u}}{b}}^{\frac{s}{b}}|K(v)|dvds=O(\Delta_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

we only need to consider
\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{t_{u}}^{T}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds
. Let us write
\tau=t_{u}
for simplicity. We exploit the decomposition

\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\tau}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tau}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds=\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\iint_{S_{\delta}}+\iint_{S_{\delta}^{c}}\right)K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds,

where
S_{\delta}=\{(s,\varpi):s-\varpi<\delta,s\in(\tau,T),\varpi\in(0,\tau)\}
for
\delta>0
and
S_{\delta}^{c}=[\tau,T]\times[0,\tau]-S_{\delta}
. Letting
w=\frac{s-\varpi}{b}
first and then
v=\frac{s}{b}
, we can write:

\begin{split}&\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\left|\iint_{S_{\delta}^{c}}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds\right|\\
&=\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\left|\int_{\tau}^{\tau+\delta}\int_{0}^{s-\delta}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds+\int_{\tau+\delta}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tau}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds\right|\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\left[\int_{\tau}^{\tau+\delta}\left(\int_{\delta/{b}}^{s/{b}}|K(w)|dw\right)ds+\int_{\tau+\delta}^{T}\left(\int_{(s-\tau)/{b}}^{s/{b}}|K(w)|dw\right)ds\right]\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\left[\int_{\tau/{b}}^{(\tau+\delta)/{b}}\left(\int_{\delta/{b}}^{v}|K(w)|dw\right)dv+\int_{(\tau+\delta)/{b}}^{T/{b}}\left(\int_{v-\tau/{b}}^{v}|K(w)|dw\right)dv\right]\\
&\leq C\left[\bar{L}(\delta/{b})\frac{\delta}{b}+\bar{L}(\delta/{b})\frac{T}{b}\right]\to 0,\end{split}

(A.42)

for any
\delta>0
, where
\bar{L}(x)=\int_{x}^{\infty}\left|K(u)\right|du
and the last equation uses
\bar{L}(x)x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle x\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
derived from
K(x)x^{2+\epsilon}\to 0
,
\epsilon>1/2
as
|x|\to\infty
.

Next, we consider the integral over
S_{\delta}.
Clearly, for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists
\delta>0
such that
\left|\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\tau})\right|<\epsilon
, for
|\varpi-\tau|<\delta
. Letting
w=\frac{s-\varpi}{b}
first and then
v=\frac{s-\tau}{b}
, we can write

\begin{split}&\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{S_{\delta}}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds\\
&=\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{\tau}^{\tau+\delta}\int_{s-\delta}^{\tau}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds\\
&\leq\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{\tau}^{\tau+\delta}\left(\int_{(s-\tau)/{b}}^{\delta/{b}}K(w)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\tau})+\epsilon\right)\mathrm{1}_{K(w)>0}dw\right)ds\\
&\quad+\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{\tau}^{\tau+\delta}\left(\int_{(s-\tau)/{b}}^{\delta/{b}}K(w)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\tau})-\epsilon\right)\mathrm{1}_{K(w)<0}dw\right)ds\\
&\leq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\left[\int_{0}^{\delta/{b}}\int_{v}^{\delta/{b}}K(w)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\tau})dwdv+\epsilon\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{v}^{\infty}|K(w)|dwdv\right].\end{split}

(A.43)

Similarly, we can find a lower bound of the form:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\left[\int_{0}^{\delta/{b}}\int_{v}^{\delta/{b}}K(w)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\tau})dwdv-\epsilon\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{v}^{\infty}|K(w)|dwdv\right].

(A.44)

Recall
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\sim\theta
and
c_{\tau}=c_{t_{u}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}c_{t}
. Taking
n\to\infty
and then
\epsilon\to 0
, we have

\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{\tau}^{T}\int_{0}^{\tau}K_{{b}}(s-\varpi)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})d\varpi ds\,\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,\theta\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\int_{0}^{\infty}L(v)dv.

(A.45)

For the second term in (
A.40
), similar to (
A.41
), we can show that

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{u}\sum_{i=u}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)-\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}\int_{t_{u}}^{T}K_{{b}}(\varpi-s)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})dsd\varpi=o_{P}(1).

(A.46)

Note that
\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}\int_{t_{u}}^{T}K_{{b}}(\varpi-s)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})dsd\varpi
is the same as the left-hand side in (
A.45
) but with
K(-w)
instead of
K(w)
. Therefore,

\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}\int_{t_{u}}^{T}K_{{b}}(\varpi-s)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{\varpi})dsd\varpi\,\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,\theta\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{v}^{\infty}K(-w)dwdv.

We conclude that

H(n)_{t}\to\theta\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(v)dv,

(A.47)

where we recall that
L(t):=\int_{t}^{\infty}K(u)du\mathbf{1}_{\{t>0\}}-\int_{-\infty}^{t}K(u)du\mathbf{1}_{\{t\leq 0\}}
.
Since
c
is a.s. continuous, (
A.37
) follows by dominated convergence theorem for stochastic integrals.

Next, we proceed to show (
A.38
). Note that

\displaystyle L-\widetilde{V}^{n,\ell m}_{3}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right)\left[c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-c_{i}^{n,\ell m}\right].

(A.48)

Let us start by rewriting the inner summation in (
A.48
). Applying the conclusion in Remark
A.1
with
\tau=t_{j-1}
,
f(x)=\left(\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{j-1})-\partial_{\ell m}g(x)\right)\left(c^{n,\ell m}_{j-1}-x^{\ell m}\right)
, and
K(-x)\mathrm{1}_{x>0}
instead of
K(x)
, we have
f(c_{j-1}^{n})=0
and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=j}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)-\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right)\left[\left(c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-c_{i}^{n,\ell m}\right)\Delta_{n}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{i=j+1}^{n+1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i-1}\right)f(c^{n}_{i-1})\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)f(c_{s})ds+D_{1}(f)+\sum_{i=j+1}^{n+1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i-1}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left(f(c^{n}_{i-1})-f(c_{s})\right)ds

\displaystyle\quad=\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)f(c_{s})ds+o_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}}\right),

(A.49)

where the order
O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}})
can be justified since, for
s\in((i-1)\Delta_{n},i\Delta_{n}]
and
i>j
,

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left|f(c^{n}_{i-1})-f(c_{s})\right|&\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}(c^{n,\ell m}_{j-1}-c^{\ell m}_{s})^{2}\mathbb{E}(\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{i-1})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{s}))^{2}}\\
&\quad+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}(\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{j-1})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{i-1}))^{2}\mathbb{E}(c^{n,\ell m}_{i-1}-c^{\ell m}_{s})^{2}}\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}(t_{i}-t_{j-1})},\end{split}

for some constant
C
, and, therefore, for some possibly different constant
C
,

\begin{split}\sum_{i=j+1}^{n+1}|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i-1}\right)|\sqrt{\Delta_{n}|t_{j-1}-t_{i}|}\Delta_{n}&\leq C\int_{t_{j+1}}^{T}|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)|\sqrt{\Delta_{n}|t_{j-1}-s|}ds\\
&\leq C\int_{-\infty}^{0}|K\left(u\right)|\sqrt{\Delta_{n}|u|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}}ds=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}}\right).\end{split}

Plugging (
A.49
) back into (
A.48
), recalling that
f(x)=\left(\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{j-1})-\partial_{\ell m}g(x)\right)\left(c^{n,\ell m}_{j-1}-x^{\ell m}\right)
, and applying integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle L-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widetilde{V}^{n,\ell m}_{3}}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left[\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t\right)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)-\partial_{\ell m}g\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{s}}\right)\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c_{s}}^{\ell m}\right)ds\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}\right)\left(\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)-\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{T}\right)\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m}-c_{T}^{\ell m}\right)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t}{b}\right)b(j)_{s}dW_{s}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t}{b}\right)a(j)_{s}ds+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle=:P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3}+o_{p}(1),

(A.50)

where, recalling the notation
\tilde{c}_{s}^{uv,pq}=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,j}\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,j}
,

\displaystyle b(j)_{s}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{pq}(c^{\ell m}_{s}-c_{j-1}^{n,pq})\partial_{pq,\ell m}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{pq}+(\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{s})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{j-1}^{n}))\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{\ell m}

\displaystyle a(j)_{s}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{pq}(c^{\ell m}_{s}-c_{j-1}^{n,pq})\partial_{pq,\ell m}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{pq}+(\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{s})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{j-1}^{n}))\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{\ell m}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{uv,pq}(c_{s}^{\ell m}-c_{j-1}^{n,\ell m})\partial^{3}_{uv,pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{uv,pq}+\sum_{pq}\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}.

For the first term, for some
\xi
in between
c_{T}
and
c_{j-1}^{n}
, we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left|P_{1}\right|&\leq\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\partial_{\ell m}g\left(\xi\right)\right\|\left\|c_{j-1}^{n}-c_{T}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}\right)\right|\left(T-t_{j-1}\right)\\
\leq&C\frac{{b}^{2}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\int_{0}^{T}|L(u)|udu\to 0,\end{split}

where we used (
A.7
-iii) in the second inequality above.

For
P_{2}
, let
\zeta^{n}_{j}=\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t}{b}\right)b(j)_{s}d{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}W_{s}}
so that
P_{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n}_{j}
. Observe that for
j^{\prime}<j
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j^{\prime}}\zeta^{n}_{j}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right]=0
because
\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]=0
and, thus,

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[P_{2}^{2}\right]&=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}\right)^{2}\right]\\
&=\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L^{2}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\|b(j)_{s}\|^{2}\right)ds\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L^{2}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\left(s-t_{j-1}\right)ds\\
&\leq C{b}^{2}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{-\infty}^{0}L^{2}\left(u\right)(-u)du\to 0,\end{split}

(A.51)

where in the first inequality we used that
\mathbb{E}\left\|b(j)_{s}\right\|^{2}\leq C\left|s-t_{j-1}\right|
, which follows from the definition of
b(j)_{s}
and (
A.7
).

For
P_{3}
, recall
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\sim\theta\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}
and note that
a(j)_{s}=O_{p}(\sqrt{\left|s-t_{j-1}\right|})+\sum_{pq}\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}

due to (
A.7
). We then have

\begin{split}P_{3}&=\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)a(j)_{s}ds\\
&=o_{p}(1)+\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}\sum_{pq}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}ds\\
&=o_{p}(1)+\sqrt{\Delta}_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\sum_{pq}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{t_{j-1}})\tilde{c}_{t_{j-1}}^{\ell m,pq}\int_{\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}}^{0}L\left(u\right)du\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\theta\sum_{pq}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}ds\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du,\end{split}

(A.52)

since

\sqrt{\Delta}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}|L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)|\sqrt{s-t_{j-1}}ds\leq C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Delta^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}|L\left(u\right)|\sqrt{-u}du=O(\Delta^{\frac{1}{4}}),

and, applying again (
A.7
),

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}ds-\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{t_{j-1}})\tilde{c}_{t_{j-1}}^{\ell m,pq}h\int_{\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}}^{0}L\left(u\right)du\right|\\
&\leq\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}\left|L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left|\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}-\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{t_{j-1}})\tilde{c}_{t_{j-1}}^{\ell m,pq}\right|ds\\
&\leq C\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}\left|L\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\right|\sqrt{s-t_{j-1}}ds\\
&\leq C{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}\int_{-\infty}^{0}|L(u)|\sqrt{-u{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b}}du=O({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b^{3/2}}),\end{split}

(A.53)

where
\int_{-\infty}^{0}|L(u)|\sqrt{-u}du<\infty
can be shown using the condition
K(x)=O(|x|^{-2-\epsilon})
, as
|x|\to\infty
, for some
\epsilon>1/2
. Indeed, for some
M<0
and
B<\infty
,

\begin{split}\int_{-\infty}^{0}|L(u)|\sqrt{-u}du&\leq\int_{M}^{0}|L(u)|\sqrt{-u}du+\int_{-\infty}^{M}\sqrt{-u}\int_{-\infty}^{u}|K(x)|dxdu\\
&\leq\int_{M}^{0}|L(u)|\sqrt{-u}du+B\int_{-\infty}^{M}\sqrt{-u}\int_{-\infty}^{u}|x|^{-2-\epsilon}dxdu<\infty.\end{split}

This concludes
L-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\widetilde{V}^{n,\ell m}_{3}}\to\theta\sum_{pq}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,\ell m}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{\ell m,pq}ds\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du
and, hence, (
A.34
).

A.2.4
The bias term
V^{n}_{4}

We now show that

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}V^{n}_{4}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,{\frac{1}{2\theta}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K^{2}\left(u\right)du}

(A.54)

\displaystyle+{\frac{\theta}{2}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sum_{r=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r}_{s}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right)}.

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}V^{n}_{4}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}v_{i}^{n}\bar{K}_{n}\left(t_{i}\right)
, where
v_{i}^{n}=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left(g\left(c_{i}^{n}+\beta_{i}^{n}\right)-g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\nabla g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\cdot\beta_{i}^{n}\right).

By Taylor’s expansion and (
2.11
), for some constants
{\ell}\geq 3
,
v_{i}^{n}=v(1)_{i}^{n}+v(2)_{i}^{n}
, where

\displaystyle v(1)_{i}^{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv},

\displaystyle|v(2)_{i}^{n}|

\displaystyle\leq{}C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}(1+\|\beta_{i}^{n}\|^{{\ell}-3})\|\beta_{i}^{n}\|^{3}.

For
v(2)^{n}_{i}
, applying (
A.11
) and recalling that
k_{n}\sim\frac{\theta}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}|v(2)^{n}_{i}|\right]\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left(k_{n}^{-3/2}+(k_{n}\Delta_{n})^{3/2}\right)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\Delta_{n}^{3/4}\to 0.

(A.55)

Thus, we only need to consider
v(1)^{n}_{i}
. Recalling (
A.2
), write

\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}=\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-2}\sum_{r=1}^{6}\xi^{n}_{{r},i},

(A.56)

where

\displaystyle\xi_{1,i}^{n}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv},

\displaystyle\xi^{n}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\xi^{n}_{3,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\alpha_{l}^{n,pq},

\displaystyle\xi^{n}_{4,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{n-1}\sum_{j=l+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\alpha_{l}^{n,pq},

\displaystyle\xi^{n}_{5,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n},

\displaystyle\xi^{n}_{6,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

Therefore, we only need to consider the convergence of

S_{n,r}:=S_{n,r}^{(p,q,u,v)}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\xi^{n}_{r,i},\quad r=1,\dots,6,

(A.57)

for each
p,q,u,v
, since

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}V^{n}_{4}}=\sum_{r=1}^{6}\sum_{p,q,u,v}S_{n,r}^{(p,q,u,v)}+o_{p}(1).

When
{r=1}
,

\begin{split}{S_{n,1}}&=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\\
&\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\\
&=:R+L.\end{split}

(A.58)

Similar to the idea behind (
A.26
), we define

M=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv},

and aim to show that

\displaystyle R+M\to{\frac{1}{2\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{0}^{\infty}K^{2}\left(u\right)du},

(A.59)

\displaystyle L-M\to 0.

(A.60)

For (
A.60
), due to (
A.7
), (
A.8
), and (
2.11
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|L-M\right|=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)|^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{j}^{n}\right\|^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)|^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(\xi\right)\right\|^{2}\left\|c_{i}^{n}-c_{j-1}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)|^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{\left|t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right|\Delta_{n}^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{-\infty}^{0}K^{2}(u)\sqrt{-u{b}}\,du\frac{1}{{b}}\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}}}\to 0,

(A.61)

given
\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)\right|>\delta
.
For (
A.59
), we write it as

\displaystyle R+M

\displaystyle=:\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n}_{j}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv},

(A.62)

where

\zeta^{n}_{j}:=\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)+\sum_{i=j}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)=:\zeta_{j}^{n,1}+\zeta_{j}^{n,2}.

In particular, note that
R+M
is an adapted triangular array, since
\zeta^{n}_{j}
and
\alpha^{n}_{j}
are measurable w.r.t.
\mathcal{F}_{j-1}
and
\mathcal{F}_{j}
, respectively. By (
A.9
), we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\big|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n}_{j}\left(\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\right),\end{split}

(A.63)

where recall that
\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}=\left(c_{s}^{pu}c_{s}^{qv}+c_{s}^{pv}c_{s}^{qu}\right)
.
We now consider the limits of

A_{n}:=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2},\quad B_{n}:=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n,2}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2}.

(A.64)

For the first term above, let us first note that we expect that

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}\left(s\right)^{-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})ds+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b_{n}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b_{n}}\right).

(A.65)

The proof of (
A.65
) can be found in Appendix
B.2
.
Therefore, recalling that
\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{{b_{n}}}\to\frac{1}{\theta}
,

\displaystyle A_{n}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\check{c}^{n,pq,uv}_{j-1}\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}\left(s\right)^{-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})ds\Delta_{n}+O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\check{c}^{n,pq,uv}_{j-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\int_{0}^{{\frac{t_{j-1}}{b}}}K^{2}(u)\left[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K\left(u+\frac{t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}}{b}\right)\right]^{-1}du\frac{1}{b}}

\displaystyle\qquad+O_{p}(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{b_{n}}}\right)

\displaystyle={\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{{b}}}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}\int_{0}^{\frac{s}{{b}}}K^{2}\left(u\right)\left[\int_{u-\frac{{s}}{{b}}}^{u+\frac{T-{s}}{{b}}}K(v)dv\right]^{-1}duds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{0}^{\infty}K^{2}\left(u\right)du,

(A.66)

where in the second equality we used that, for
j=1,2,\cdots,n
,

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}(s)^{-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})ds-\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}(s)^{-1}ds\right|\\
&\quad\leq\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}(s)^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left|\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})-\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\right|ds\\
&\quad\leq C\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}(s)^{-1}\sqrt{\left|s-t_{j-1}\right|}ds\\
&\quad\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{1}{b}\sqrt{b}\int_{0}^{\infty}K^{2}(u)\sqrt{u}du=O\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}\right),\end{split}

(A.67)

where we used (
A.7
) and (
2.11
).
For the second term in (
A.64
), similar to (
A.65
), we have

\sum_{i=j}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}-\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t\right)\bar{K}\left(t\right)^{-1}dt=O_{p}(1),

and, thus,

\displaystyle{B_{n}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\check{c}^{n,pq,uv}_{j-1}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}\left(s\right)^{-1}ds\Delta_{n}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\check{c}^{n,pq,uv}_{j-1}\int_{\frac{t_{j-1}-T}{b}}^{0}K^{2}\left(u\right)\left[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K\left(u+\frac{t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}}{b}\right)\right]^{-1}du\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}\int_{\frac{s-T}{b}}^{0}K^{2}\left(u\right)\left[\int_{u-\frac{s}{b}}^{u+\frac{T-s}{b}}K(v)dv\right]^{-1}duds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}^{pq,uv}_{s}ds\int_{-\infty}^{0}K^{2}\left(u\right)du.

(A.68)

For the contribution of the second term on the right-hand side of (
A.63
), note that

\displaystyle|\zeta^{n}_{j}|\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{1}{b_{n}\Delta_{n}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K^{2}\left(u\right)du.

Therefore,

\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}O\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{n}|\zeta^{n}_{j}|\leq\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}O\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\frac{1}{b_{n}\Delta_{n}^{2}}\leq O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b_{n}}\right)=o_{p}(1).

(A.69)

Finally, again using
|\zeta_{j}^{n}|=O_{p}(b_{n}^{-1}\Delta_{n}^{-1})
and (
A.8
), we can easily show that

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[(\zeta^{n}_{j}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv})^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{C}\Delta_{n}|\zeta^{n}_{j}|^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\alpha_{j}^{n}\|^{4}\big|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{5}}{b^{2}\Delta_{n}^{3}}\right)=o_{p}(1).

(A.70)

Combining with (
A.61
), (
A.62
), (
A.66
), (
A.68
), and (
A.70
), we conclude that

S_{n,1}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\xi^{n}_{1,i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{2\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K^{2}\left(u\right)du.

(A.71)

For
{r=2}
in (
A.57
), first note that, by Lemma
A.1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n}-\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{s}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(c_{i-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta K}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+o\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\right),

(A.72)

where
\Delta K:=\left(K\left(A^{+}\right)-K\left(B^{-}\right)\right)
.
Thus,

\displaystyle S_{n,2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{5}{2}}}{2}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Bigg[\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{s}^{{pq}}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)ds+\frac{1}{2}\left(c_{i-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta K}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+o\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{s}^{{uv}}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)ds+\frac{1}{2}\left(c_{i-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta K}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+o\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)dt\right)\left(\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)dt\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)dt\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{4b}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{i-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta K}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)dt\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{4b}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{i-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta K}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=:P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3}+o_{p}(1),

(A.73)

where

\begin{split}P_{2}&=O\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)dt\right|\right),\\
P_{3}&=O\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)dt\right|\right).\end{split}

(A.74)

The above estimate follows from the boundedness of
K
, (
2.11
), (
A.1
), (
A.13
), and the convergence of
\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}
, which is
\frac{1}{\theta}
according to (
2.12
). By integration by parts,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{t}^{pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)dt

\displaystyle\quad=-L\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{{b}}\right)\left(c_{T}^{pq}-c_{t_{i}}^{pq}\right)+L\left(\frac{-t_{i}}{{b}}\right)\left(c_{0}^{pq}-c_{t_{i}}^{pq}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{{b}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\quad=:R^{pq}_{1,i}+R^{pq}_{2,i}+\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}}).

(A.75)

Now we consider each term in
P_{1}
. By (
A.7
),

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}R_{1,i}^{pq}R_{1,i}^{uv}\right|\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(R_{1,i}^{pq})^{2}]\mathbb{E}[(R_{1,i}^{uv})^{2}]}\\
&\quad\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}L\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\left(T-{t_{i}}\right)\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{{b}^{2}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)^{2}udu\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0,\end{split}

(A.76)

where above we used that
\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)^{2}udu<\infty
, which can be deduced from the condition
K(x)x^{2+\epsilon}\to 0
, as
|x|\to\infty
, for
\epsilon>1/2
.
In the same way, we can show that

\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}R_{2,i}^{pq}R_{2,i}^{uv}\right|\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{{b}^{2}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)^{2}|u|du\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

(A.77)

For the mixed term
|R_{1,i}^{pq}R_{2,i}^{uv}|
, we simply upper bound it by
2^{-1}(|R_{1,i}^{pq}|^{2}+|R_{2,i}^{uv}|^{2})
and proceed as above.
For the term of
P_{1}
corresponding to the third term in (
A.75
), note that, using that
\tilde{\sigma}
and
c
are bounded, (
2.11
), and BDG inequality, for
t_{i}<\sqrt{b}
,

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}\right)\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{uv}_{t}dW_{t}\right)\right|\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}\right)^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}dt\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\int L\left(u\right)^{2}dub\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{b\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sqrt{b}}{\Delta_{n}}\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{b}\to 0.\end{split}

For
t_{i}>\sqrt{b}
, similar to the proof of Theorem 6.2 in
Figueroa-López & Li (2020)
,
\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}_{t}dW_{t}=\sum_{r=1}^{d}\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dW^{r}_{t}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}})
and, thus, for any
r_{1},r_{2}\in\{1,\dots,d\}
, it suffices to consider the convergence of

\displaystyle\sum_{i:t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\,\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{2}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dW^{r_{1}}_{t}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dW^{r_{2}}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad=:\sum_{i:t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\zeta^{n,r_{1},r_{2}}_{i}+o_{p}(1),

(A.78)

where

\zeta^{n,r_{1},r_{2}}_{i}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{2}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dW^{r_{1}}_{t}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dW^{r_{2}}_{t}.

For simplicity, we write
\zeta^{n}_{i}
instead of
\zeta^{n,r_{1},r_{2}}_{i}
. For the convergence of (
A.78
), note that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta^{n}_{i}\right|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]=0
if
r_{1}\neq r_{2}
, but, when
r_{2}=r_{1}
,

\begin{split}&\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta^{n}_{i}\right|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]=\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}dt\\
&=\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{1}}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{\frac{T-t_{i}}{{b}}}L\left(u\right)^{2}du{b}\\
&=\int_{0}^{T-\sqrt{b}}\frac{\theta}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\left(\int_{-\frac{s+\sqrt{b}}{{b}}}^{\frac{T-s-\sqrt{b}}{{b}}}K(v)dv\right)^{-1}\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{1}}_{s}\left(\int_{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{\frac{T-s-\sqrt{b}}{{b}}}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right)ds+o_{p}(1)\\
&\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,\frac{\theta}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{1}}_{s}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right),\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\left(\zeta^{n}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{i}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]\right)\right)^{2}&=\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\mathbb{E}\left(\zeta^{n}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{i}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]\right)^{2}\\
&\quad\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta^{n}_{i}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]\right]\\
&\quad\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\Delta_{n}\left(\int_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}dt\right)^{2}\\
&\quad\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\Delta_{n}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right)^{2}{b}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.\end{split}

Therefore, we obtain that, as
n\to\infty
,

\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\zeta^{n,r_{1},r_{2}}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\theta}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r_{1}}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r_{1}}_{s}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right){\bf 1}_{r_{1}=r_{2}}.

(A.79)

Therefore, combining the previous inequalities, we obtain

P_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,\frac{\theta}{2}\sum_{r=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r}_{s}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right).

(A.80)

As for
P_{2}
and
P_{3}
, by (
A.74
) and (
A.75
), since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}R_{1,i}^{pq}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left|L\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}\right)\left(c_{T}^{pq}-c_{t_{i}}^{pq}\right)\right|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\left|L\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\left(T-t_{i}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}|L(u)|b\sqrt{bu}du=b^{3/2}\int_{0}^{\infty}|L(u)|\sqrt{u}du\rightarrow 0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t}^{pq}dW_{t}\right|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t}^{pq}dW_{t}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}dt}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u)^{2}dub}\rightarrow 0,

we have
P_{2}\to 0
. Along the same steps,
P_{3}\to 0
as well. Thus, as
n\to\infty
,

S_{n,2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\,\frac{\theta}{2}\sum_{r=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{s})\,\tilde{\sigma}^{pq,r}_{s}\tilde{\sigma}^{uv,r}_{s}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L\left(u\right)^{2}du\right).

(A.81)

For
r=3
in (
A.57
), we consider the decomposition

\begin{split}S_{n,3}&=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\\
&\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{l=j+1}^{i}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\\
&\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{l=i+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\\
&=:R+L+Cross.\end{split}

(A.82)

We can write

\begin{split}R=\sum_{l=3}^{n}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}=:\sum_{l=3}^{n}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l},\end{split}

which is now an adapted triangular array where
\zeta^{n}_{l}
is
\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}
measurable.
Then, by (
A.8
), (
A.9
), and (
A.4
),

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}\right]\right|\leq C\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[|\zeta^{n}_{l}|\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)\right]\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\Delta_{n}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}|K(x)|dx\right)^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{\sum_{l=3}^{n}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}|K(x)|dx\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.\end{split}

We also have, for
j<j^{\prime}
,

\begin{split}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,uv}_{j^{\prime}}\right)\right|&=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j^{\prime}}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j^{\prime}-1}\right)\right]\right|\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j^{\prime}}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j^{\prime}-1}\right)^{2}\right)}\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}^{3}\mathbb{E}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{j^{\prime}-1}\right)^{2}}\leq C\Delta_{n}^{5/2}.\end{split}

(A.83)

Using this inequality and (
A.8
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\left(\zeta^{n}_{l}\right)^{2}]\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\right]\\
&\quad+C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\left(\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\max_{j,j^{\prime}:j\neq j^{\prime}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,uv}_{j^{\prime}}\right)\right|\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}b\sum_{l=3}^{n}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|K(u)K(v+u)\right|du\right)^{2}dv\\
&\quad+C\sum_{l=3}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|K(u)K(v+u)\right|dudv\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0,\end{split}

(A.84)

where the finiteness of the integrals above can be shown using the condition
K(x)x^{2+\epsilon}\to 0
, for
\epsilon>1/2
, as
|x|\to\infty
and boundedness of
K
.
Hence, by Lemma 2.2.12 in
Jacod & Protter (2011)
,
R=\sum_{l=2}^{n}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathbb{P}}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
.
Similarly, we can write the term
L
in (
A.82
) as

\begin{split}L&=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n}_{j}.\end{split}

Note that (see proof in Appendix
B.2
)

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right),\text{ for }i\geq l,

(A.85)

with
\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}=\max\left(\eta(Y)_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n},Y=\mu,\tilde{\mu},c,\tilde{c},\hat{c}\right).

Now consider

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{j}^{n}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\\
&=\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\\
&\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{l-1}\sum_{l=2}^{i}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left|\alpha_{j}^{n,nv}\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)\right|\\
&\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{l-1}\sum_{l=2}^{i}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Delta_{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{j}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{l-1}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Delta_{n}\sum_{l=2}^{i}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\left(\sum_{i=l}^{\left(l+\sqrt{b}/\Delta_{n}-1\right)\wedge n}+\sum_{i=\left(l+\sqrt{b}/\Delta_{n}\right)\wedge n}^{n}\right)\left|K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&\leq Cb\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left\{\int_{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{0}\left|K(u)\right|(-u)du\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{l-1,\frac{\sqrt{b}}{\Delta_{n}}}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]}+\int_{\left(\frac{t_{l-1}-T}{b}\right)\wedge-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}\left|K(u)\right|(-u)du\right\},\end{split}

(A.86)

where

\int_{\left(\frac{t_{l-1}-T}{b}\right)\wedge-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}\left|K(u)\right|(-u)du\to 0,\quad\int_{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{0}\left|K(u)\right|(-u)du<\infty.

Observe that, from the notation in (
A.3
),

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{l-1,\frac{\sqrt{b}}{\Delta_{n}}}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\eta_{t_{l-1},\sqrt{b}}\right)^{2}\right]\to 0,

when
\sqrt{b}\to 0
, because
\eta_{t_{l-1},\sqrt{b}}\leq C
and the càdlàg property of
c,\tilde{\mu},\tilde{c}
implies that
\eta_{s,\sqrt{b}}\to 0
for all
s
except for countably many positive values of
s
(see also
Jacod & Rosenbaum (2013)
, proof of Lemma 4.2). Plugging back in (
A.86
), we obtain

\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|\to 0.

(A.87)

Next, note that

\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}\right)^{2}\right],

and, therefore,

\begin{aligned} &\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\\
&\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}\right]\\
&\leq{\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\right)^{2}}\\
&\quad+(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j})^{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,l^{\prime}=j+1\\
l\neq l^{\prime}\end{subarray}}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\right)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\left(\sum_{i^{\prime}=l^{\prime}}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i^{\prime}}\right)\right)\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l^{\prime}}\Bigg]\\
&\leq C\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{4}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=l}^{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{2}\\
&\quad+C\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{5}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l,l^{\prime}=j+1\\
l\neq l^{\prime}\end{subarray}}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=l}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\left|\sum_{i^{\prime}=l^{\prime}}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t_{i^{\prime}}}\right)K_{{b}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}}\right)\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l^{\prime}+1}\right)^{2}}(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l^{\prime}-1})\right|\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}\int_{-\frac{T}{b}}^{0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s}^{0}}\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s-v}^{0}}\left|K(v+u)K(u)\right|du\right)^{2}dvds\\
&+Cb^{2}\int_{-\frac{T}{b}}^{0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s}^{0}}\left|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s-v}^{0}}K(v+u)K(u)du\right|dv\right.\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\left.{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s}^{0}}\left|{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\int_{s-v^{\prime}}^{0}}K(v^{\prime}+u^{\prime})K(u^{\prime})u^{\prime}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta_{T+(s-v)b,|u^{\prime}|b}\right)^{2}}du^{\prime}\right|dv^{\prime}\right\}ds\longrightarrow 0,\end{aligned}

(A.88)

where in the third inequality we use the following estimate valid for
i^{\prime}\geq i\geq l\geq l^{\prime}\geq j
,

\begin{split}&\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i^{\prime}}\right)\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l^{\prime}}\right]\right|\\
&\quad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l^{\prime}}\right|\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c^{n}_{i^{\prime}}\right)\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}\right]\right|\right]\\
&\quad\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l^{\prime}}\right|\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l+1}(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l-1})\right]\\
&\quad\leq C\Delta_{n}^{4}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l+1}\right)^{2}}(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l-1})\\
&\quad\leq C\Delta_{n}^{4}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l^{\prime}+1}\right)^{2}}(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l^{\prime}-1}).\end{split}

(A.89)

The second inequality above is shown in Appendix
B.2
and the third inequality in (
A.89
) follows by (
A.8
) and Cauchy–Schwarz inequality. Finally, the Cross term of (
A.82
) can be analyzed similarly to the case of
L
in (
A.82
). We then conclude

S_{n,r}\to_{p}0,\quad\text{for}\ r=3\ \text{and}\ r=4.

(A.90)

For
{r=5}
, first note that, by successive conditioning and Itô’s lemma, we can prove that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|

(A.91)

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i,l-1<j,\\
C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{j-1,l-j}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i<j\leq l-1,\\
C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{j-1,i-j+1}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i\geq j>l-1,\\
C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,l-j}^{n}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{j-1,i-j+1}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i,l-1\geq j.\end{cases}

The proof of (
A.91
) is also included in Appendix
B.2
. With these inequalities, when
r=5
,

\displaystyle S_{n,5}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n}.

By (
A.91
), we can estimate
M:=\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|
as follows:

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{j-1,\left|l-j\right|}^{n}\right]\left|t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right|+\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1,\left|i-j+1\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\int_{-t_{i}/b}^{(T-t_{i})/b}\left|K(u)\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right]\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}+ub\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}+ub\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\Big]du

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{(t_{j-1}-T)/b}^{t_{j-1}/b}\left|K(v)\right|\int_{v-\frac{t_{j-1}}{b}}^{v+\frac{T-t_{j-1}}{b}}\left|K(u)\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|u-v\right|b}\right]\left|u-v\right|b+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v\right|b}\right]\left|v\right|b\Big]dudv

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}b\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int\left|K(v)\right|\int\left|K(u)\right|\Bigg[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|u-v\right|b}\right]\left|u-v\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v\right|b}\right]\left|v\right|\Bigg]dudv\to 0.

Also, by (
A.7
) and (
A.8
), and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\sum_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\times\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\left|c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\left|c_{l^{\prime}-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i^{\prime}}^{n,pq}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{5}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\sum_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\right|\left|t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right|^{1/4}\left|t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right|^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\int\left|K(v)\right|\left|v\right|^{1/4}dv\right)^{2}\left(\int\left|K(u)\right|du\right)^{2}\sqrt{b}\to 0.

Thus,
{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(5)\to_{p}0
. We handle the case
r=6
in the same way as
r=5
. Now, we can finally conclude (
A.90
) for
r=5,6
and then (
A.54
).

A.2.5
The bias term
V^{n}_{5}

We now show that

V^{n}_{5}\to\theta\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)dug\left(c_{0}\right)-\theta\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)dug\left(c_{T}\right).

(A.92)

By Assumption
2
,
K^{\prime}
and
K^{\prime\prime}
are defined in all
(A,B)\subset\mathbb{R}
, but finite number of points. Without loss of generality, suppose
\operatorname{dom}(K^{\prime})=\operatorname{dom}(K^{\prime\prime})=(A,B)\backslash\{0\}
.
Note that

\displaystyle\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-1

\displaystyle=-\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t_{i}/{b}}+\int_{\left(T-t_{i}\right)/{b}}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{b}\int_{0}^{T}K\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt

\displaystyle=:-\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t_{i}/{b}}+\int_{\left(T-t_{i}\right)/{b}}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du+D_{1}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right).

Then,

\displaystyle V_{5}^{n}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-1\right]g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

(A.93)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[-\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t_{i}/{b}}+\int_{\left(T-t_{i}\right)/{b}}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du+D_{1}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)\right]g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=:-I-II+III,

where note that
D_{1}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)
can be further decomposed as follows, for some
s_{t}\in\left(t_{j-1},t_{j}\right)
,

\displaystyle D_{1}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{1}{b}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left(K\Big(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\Big)-K\Big(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\Big)\right)dt}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\frac{t_{j-1}-t}{b}dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{b}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{t}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]\frac{t_{j-1}-t}{b}dt

\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}K\left(-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}K\left(0\right)-\frac{1}{b}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i+1}}K\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt

\displaystyle=:D_{11}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)+D_{12}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)+D_{13}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right).

With the notation
\tilde{K}(u)=\int_{-\infty}^{-u}K(v)dv
and applying Lemma
A.1
, we have:

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{-\infty}^{-t_{i}/{b}}K(u)dug\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{K}_{b}\left(t\right)g\left(c_{t}\right)dt+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}O_{P}\Big(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\Big)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}-\theta\int_{\infty}^{0}L(u)dug(c_{0}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0},}

where in the last limit we used that
b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\to\theta
.
Similarly, let
K^{*}(u)=\int^{\infty}_{-u}K(v)dv=1-\tilde{K}(u)
. Then,

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\left(T-t_{i}\right)/{b}}^{\infty}K(u)dug\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)=b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{b}K^{*}\left(\frac{t_{i}-T}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{0}^{T}K^{*}_{b}(t-T)g\left(c_{t}\right)dt+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}b\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}O_{P}\Big(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\Big)}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\theta\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)dug(c_{T}).

As for
III
, by the same argument as in (
B.7
) & (
B.8
) with
f\equiv 1
, we have
D_{12}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)=O\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}}{{b}^{2}}\right)
. For
D_{13}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)
, by the continuity of
K(x)
at
x=0
, we have
K\left(-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+K\left(0\right)-\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i+1}}K\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt\to 0
when
n\to\infty
. Thus,

\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{13}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\to_{p}0.

For
D_{11}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)
, consider

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{11}^{K,t_{i}}\left(1\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)=\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

(A.94)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\left(\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}+\int_{t_{i+1}}^{T}\right)\frac{1}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt\right]g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}+\int_{t_{i+1}}^{T}\right)\frac{1}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dtg\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)=:IV+V.

Clearly,

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\int_{-\frac{t_{i}}{b}}^{-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}}+\int_{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}}^{\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}}\right)K^{\prime}(v)dvg\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[K\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)+K\left(-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right).

Note that with the notation
\tilde{K}^{*}(u)=K(-u)
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{-t_{i}}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{K}^{*}\left(\frac{t_{i}}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

(A.95)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{K}^{*}_{{b}}(t)g\left(c_{t}\right)dt+D^{\tilde{K}^{*},0}_{1}(g)\right]=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\left[\int_{0}^{T/{b}}\tilde{K}(u)g\left(c_{ub}\right)du+D^{\tilde{K}^{*},0}_{1}(g)\right]\to_{p}0,

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{T-t_{i}}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{K}^{*}\left(\frac{t_{i}-T}{b}\right)g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)

(A.96)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\left[\int_{0}^{T}\tilde{K}^{*}_{{b}}(t-T)g\left(c_{t}\right)dt+D^{\tilde{K}^{*},T}_{1}(g)\right]=\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\left[\int^{0}_{-T/{b}}\tilde{K}(u)g\left(c_{uh+T}\right)du+D^{\tilde{K}^{*},T}_{1}(g)\right]\to_{p}0.

By (
A.95
) & (
A.96
), together with the fact that
K\left(-\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)\to 0
when
n\to\infty
, we get
V\to_{p}0
. For
IV
, note that for
j\neq i,i+1
,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}|A_{j}|}

\displaystyle:=\left|\frac{1}{2}\left[K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)+K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j}-t_{i}\right)\right]-\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t-t_{i}\right)dt\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j}-t_{i}\right)-K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t-t_{i}\right)\right]-\left[K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t-t_{i}\right)-K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right]dt\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[\int_{t}^{t_{j}}\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)ds-\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t}\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)ds\right]dt\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[\int_{t_{j-1}}^{s}\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt-\int_{s}^{t_{j}}\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt\right]ds\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[\left(s-t_{j-1}\right)\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\left(t_{j}-s\right)\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]ds\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left(s-\frac{t_{j-1}+t_{j}}{2}\right)\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\left|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left(s-\frac{t_{j-1}+t_{j}}{2}\right)\left[\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{b^{2}}K^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{\frac{t_{j-1}+t_{j}}{2}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b^{2}\Delta_{n}}\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left|s-\frac{t_{j-1}+t_{j}}{2}\right|ds=\frac{\Delta_{n}}{4b^{2}}\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\right),

where
\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\delta\right)=\sup\left\{\left|K^{\prime\prime}\left(s\right)-K^{\prime\prime}\left(t\right)\right|:\left|s-t\right|\leq\delta\right\}
is the modulus of continuity of
K^{\prime\prime}
. Note that
\lim_{\delta\downarrow 0}\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}(\delta)=0
is true since we are assuming that
K^{\prime\prime}
is uniformly continuous on its domain
\operatorname{dom}(K^{\prime\prime})=(A,B)\backslash\{0\}
. Recalling Assumption
2
and that
b_{n}\sim\theta\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}
, we have
\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\to 0
, and thus
\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\right)\to 0
. Then, sum it up (except for
j=i,i+1
) and we will obtain

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Big|\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}A_{j}\Big|}

\displaystyle=\Big|\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)-\frac{K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(-t_{i}\right)-K_{b}^{\prime}\left(-\Delta_{n}\right)+K_{b}^{\prime}\left(\Delta_{n}\right)-K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(T-t_{i}\right)}{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{\Delta_{n}}\left(\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}+\int_{t_{i+1}}^{T}\right)K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(s-t_{i}\right)ds\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T}{4b^{2}}\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle\left|\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)-\left(\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}+\int_{t_{i+1}}^{T}\right)K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(s-t_{i}\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{n}\left|\frac{K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(-t_{i}\right)-K_{b}^{\prime}\left(-\Delta_{n}\right)+K_{b}^{\prime}\left(\Delta_{n}\right)-K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(T-t_{i}\right)}{2}\right|+\frac{T\Delta_{n}}{4b^{2}}\omega_{K^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{2b}\right)\to 0.

Therefore,

\displaystyle|IV|

\displaystyle=\Big|\frac{\Delta_{n}^{3/2}}{2b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1,j\neq i,i+1}^{n}K_{b}^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\left(\int_{0}^{t_{i-1}}+\int_{t_{i+1}}^{T}\right)\frac{1}{b}K_{b}^{\prime}\left(\frac{t-t_{i}}{b}\right)dt\right]g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\Big|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Combining all the results and plugging back in (
A.93
) & (
A.94
), (
A.92
) is proved.

A.2.6
Eliminating
\theta\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{\ell,m=1}^{d}\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{s}\right)dc^{lm}_{s}

From (
A.24
), (
A.34
), (
A.54
), and (
A.92
) we deduce that

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\Big(V(g)_{T}^{n}-V(g)_{T}\Big)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle st}}{{\longrightarrow}}\widetilde{A}^{1}+\widetilde{A}^{2}+\widetilde{A}^{3}+Z,

(A.97)

with

\begin{split}\widetilde{A}^{1}&:=\theta{\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du}g\left(c_{0}\right)-\theta{\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)du}g\left(c_{T}\right),\\
\widetilde{A}^{2}&:=\frac{1}{2\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K^{2}(u)du,\\
\widetilde{A}^{3}&:=\theta\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{\ell,m=1}^{d}\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{s}\right)dc^{lm}_{s}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u)du\\
&\quad+\theta\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}^{pq,uv}_{s}ds\left[\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du+\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u)^{2}du\right]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}.}\end{split}

On the other hand, by Itô’s formula, we have

\displaystyle g\left(c_{T}\right)

\displaystyle=g\left(c_{0}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{l,m}\partial_{lm}g\left(c_{s}\right)dc_{s}^{lm}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds.

Hence, solving for the second term and plugging in the expression of
\widetilde{A}^{3}
, we can rewrite the bias
\widetilde{A}^{1}+\widetilde{A}^{2}+\widetilde{A}^{3}
as
{A}^{1}+{A}^{2}+{A}^{3}
with the terms
A^{j}
’s given as in
(
2.15
).

A.3
Proof of Theorem
2.2

We first discuss the elimination of the jumps. Suppose
X
is discontinuous as in (
2.1
) and
\hat{c}^{n}_{i}
in (
2.23
) is given by (
2.6
). Let

\widehat{A}^{\prime n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n})\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n,uv}\right),

(A.98)

where
\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}=\hat{c}_{i}^{n,X^{\prime}}
is defined in (
2.4
) with the continuous process
X^{\prime}
defined in (
A.5
). We prove here that

\widehat{A}^{n,3}-\widehat{A}^{\prime n,3}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}0.

(A.99)

Let

R\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n},\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}\right)=\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,uv}\right).

By (
2.11
) and the mean value theorem, for some
\xi\in(0,1)
,

\begin{split}&\left|R\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n},\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}\right)-R\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n},\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n}\right)\right|\\
&\leq\left|\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\left[\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,uv}\right)-\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n,uv}\right)\right]\right|\\
&+\left|\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n})\right]\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\\
&\leq C\left(1+\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ell}-2}\right)\left(\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right\|+\left\|\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}\right\|\right)\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right\|+\left\|\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n}\right\|}\right)\\
&+C\left(1+\left\|\xi\hat{c}_{i}^{n}+\left(1-\xi\right)\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right\|^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\ell}-3}\right)\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right\|\left(\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right\|+\left\|\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}\right\|\right)^{2}.\end{split}

(A.100)

By (
A.16
) with
q=1
and
q^{\prime}
large enough, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right\|^{q}\right]\leq Ca_{n}\Delta_{n}^{(2-r)\varpi}.

It is easy to see that under (
2.8
),
(2-r)\varpi\geq{}1/2
when
\ell\geq{}4
and thus, we conclude that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|R\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n},\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}\right)-R\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n},\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{\prime n}\right)\right|\right]=o(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}),

which implies (
A.99
). Now suppose
X
is continuous, i.e.,
X=X^{\prime}
. Then, (
A.99
) can be obtained again with (
A.18
) and (
A.100
). Hence, in what follows, we only need to consider
\hat{c}_{i}^{n}
as (
2.4
) under the Assumption
3
.

Set

\gamma_{i}^{n}:=\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n}=\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n}-\beta_{i}^{n}+c_{i+k_{n}}^{n}-c_{i}^{n}.

The estimator
\widehat{A}^{n,3}
can then be written as

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{n,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\gamma_{i}^{n,pq}\gamma_{i}^{n,uv},

where
\gamma_{i}^{n,pq}\gamma_{i}^{n,uv}
can be decomposed as

\displaystyle\gamma_{i}^{n,pq}\gamma_{i}^{n,uv}

\displaystyle=\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}+\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}-\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}

(A.101)

\displaystyle+\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)

\displaystyle-\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)-\beta_{i}^{n,uv}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)

\displaystyle+\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)+\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right).

We can then consider the decomposition:

\widehat{A}^{n,3}=\widehat{A}_{0,0}^{n,3}+\widehat{A}_{1,1}^{n,3}-\widehat{A}_{0,1}^{n,3}-\widehat{A}_{1,0}^{n,3}+\widehat{A}_{1}^{n,3}-\widehat{A}_{2}^{n,3}-\widehat{A}_{3}^{n,3}+\widehat{A}_{4}^{n,3}+\widehat{A}_{5}^{n,3},

where

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{n,3}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\beta_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv},

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{1}^{n,3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{2}^{n,3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{3}^{n,3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,uv}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{4}^{n,3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{5}^{n,3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right).

In the Appendix
B.3
, we show the following limits:

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{0,0}^{n,3},\widehat{A}_{1,1}^{n,3}

\displaystyle\to\frac{1}{8\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int^{\infty}_{-\infty}K(z)^{2}dz

(A.102)

\displaystyle+\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(z)^{2}dz,

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{0,1}^{n,3},\widehat{A}_{1,0}^{n,3}

\displaystyle\to\frac{1}{8\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int^{\infty}_{-\infty}K(z)K(z-1)dz

\displaystyle+\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(z)L(z-1)dz,

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{1}^{n,3}\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds,

(A.103)

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{2}^{n,3},\widehat{A}_{3}^{n,3}\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}K(z)dz+\int_{0}^{1}K(z)zdz\right),

(A.104)

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{4}^{n,3},\widehat{A}_{5}^{n,3}\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{0}^{1}K(z-1)(z-1)dz-\int_{-\infty}^{0}K(z-1)dz\right).

(A.105)

We finally conclude (
2.24
).

A.4
Proof of Corollary
2.1

To show (
2.26
), by Theorem
2.1
, we only need to eliminate the bias terms
A^{1},A^{2},A^{3}
. Note that

k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}g\left(\hat{c}_{1}^{n}\right){\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)du}-k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}g\left(\hat{c}_{n}^{n}\right){\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du}\to\theta g\left(c_{0}\right)\int_{0}^{\infty}L(u)du-\theta g\left(c_{T}\right)\int_{-\infty}^{0}L(u)du,

which cancels the term
A^{1}
. Then, by (
2.22
) and Theorem
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(\hat{c}_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n,uv}\right)C_{K,1}=-2C_{K,1}\widehat{A}^{n,3}

\displaystyle\to-\frac{1}{2\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j,k,l,m}\partial^{2}_{jk,lm}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{jk,lm}dsC_{K,2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\theta}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j,k,l,m}\partial^{2}_{jk,lm}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}_{s}^{jk,lm}ds\left[\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(z)^{2}dz+\left(\int_{-\infty}^{0}-\int_{0}^{\infty}\right)L(z)dz\right],

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}V\left(h\right)^{n}_{T}\left[C_{K,2}-\int K^{2}(u)du\right]\to\frac{1}{2\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left[C_{K,2}-\int K^{2}(u)du\right].

Combining both limits, the bias terms
A^{2}
and
A^{3}
can be eliminated.

A.5
Proof of Theorem
2.3

Suppose the
X
is discontinuous and
\hat{c}_{i}^{n}
in (
2.31
) is given by (
2.6
). Let

\bar{V}(g)^{\prime n}_{T}=\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g(\hat{c}^{\prime n}_{i})-\frac{1}{2k_{n}}h(\hat{c}^{\prime n}_{i})\int K^{2}(u)du\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right),

(A.106)

where
\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}=\hat{c}_{i}^{n,X^{\prime}}
is defined as in (
2.4
) with the continuous process
X^{\prime}
defined as in (
A.5
). As shown in (
A.21
), we have
\mathbb{E}\left[\left|g\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right)-g\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right)\right|\right]=o(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})
. Following the same arguments, we can show that
\mathbb{E}\left[\left|h\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right)-h\left(\hat{c}_{i}^{\prime n}\right)\right|\right]=o(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}})
. Therefore, we can conclude that

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\left[\bar{V}(g)^{n}_{T}-\bar{V}(g)^{\prime n}_{T}\right]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}0,

which, in light of b) in Lemma
A.2
, also holds when
X
is continuous under the weaker condition (
2.7
). Therefore, in what follows, we only need to consider
\hat{c}_{i}^{n}
as in (
2.4
) with Assumption
3
.

Similar to (
A.22
), we have the decomposition

\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\left(\bar{V}(g)_{T}^{n}-V(g)_{T}\right)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}{V^{n}_{j}}+{\bar{V}^{n}_{4}}+{V^{n}_{5}},

where
V^{n}_{1},V^{n}_{2},V^{n}_{3}
, and
V^{n}_{5}
are defined as in (
A.23
) and

{\bar{V}^{n}_{4}}=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g\left(c_{i}^{n}+\beta_{i}^{n}\right)-g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\nabla g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\beta_{i}^{n}-\frac{1}{2k_{n}}h\left(c^{n}_{i}+\beta^{n}_{i}\right)\int K^{2}(u)du\right).

Since
k_{n}^{2}\Delta_{n}\to{0}
corresponds to (
2.12
) with
\theta=0
, we can deduce from (
A.34
) that
V^{n}_{3}\to 0
. The asymptotic behavior of the terms
V^{n}_{1}
and
V^{n}_{2}
remain the same:
V^{n}_{1}\to 0
and
V^{n}_{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathcal{L}-\mathrm{s}}}{{\Longrightarrow}}Z
.

We now consider the updated term
\bar{V}^{n}_{4}
. We can write for some
\lambda_{i}\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle\bar{V}^{n}_{4}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}-\frac{1}{2k_{n}}h\left(c^{n}_{i}+\beta^{n}_{i}\right)\int K^{2}(u)du

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}\sum_{p_{1},q_{1},p_{2},q_{2},p_{3},q_{3}=1}^{d}\partial_{p_{1}q_{1},p_{2}q_{2},p_{3}q_{3}}^{3}g\left(c^{n}_{i}+\lambda_{i}\beta^{n}_{i}\right)\beta_{i}^{n,p_{1}q_{1}}\beta_{i}^{n,p_{2}q_{2}}\beta_{i}^{n,p_{3}q_{3}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}-\frac{1}{2k_{n}}h\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\int K^{2}(u)du

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2k_{n}}\left[h\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)_{i}-h\left(c^{n}_{i}+\beta^{n}_{i}\right)\right]\int K^{2}(u)du

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{6}\sum_{p_{1},q_{1},p_{2},q_{2},p_{3},q_{3}=1}^{d}\partial_{p_{1}q_{1},p_{2}q_{2},p_{3}q_{3}}^{3}g\left(c^{n}_{i}+\lambda_{i}\beta^{n}_{i}\right)\beta_{i}^{n,p_{1}q_{1}}\beta_{i}^{n,p_{2}q_{2}}\beta_{i}^{n,p_{3}q_{3}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i=1}^{n}{(v(1)_{i}^{n}+v(2)_{i}^{n})},

where

\begin{array}[]{l}v(1)_{i}^{n}=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\left[\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\beta_{i}^{n,uv}-\frac{1}{k_{n}}\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\int K^{2}(u)du\right]\\
\left|v(2)_{i}^{n}\right|\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left(1+\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|\right)^{{\ell}-3}\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{3}+\frac{C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{k_{n}}\left(1+\left\|\beta^{n}_{i}\right\|^{{\ell}-1}\right)\left\|\beta^{n}_{i}\right\|.\end{array}

(A.107)

Here, we used (
2.11
) and the boundedness of
c
.
For
v(2)^{n}_{i}
, by (
2.32
),
k^{2}_{n}\Delta_{n}\to 0
, we have
k^{-1}_{n}\gg k_{n}\Delta_{n}
. Then, (
A.11
) becomes

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{q}\right]\leq C_{q}k_{n}^{-q/2}.

Applying this inequality and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|v(2)_{i}^{n}\right|
