\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1+\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|\right)^{\ell-3}\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{3}\right]+\frac{C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{k_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1+\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{\ell-1}\right)\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{6}\right]\right)^{1/2}+\frac{C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{k_{n}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}k_{n}^{-3/2},

based on which, we have

\sum_{i=1}^{n}v(2)^{n}_{i}\to 0

since by (
2.32
),
k^{-3/2}_{n}\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\to 0
. For
v(1)^{n}_{i}
, recall (
A.56
), and, since
\theta=0
, (
A.81
), (
A.90
), and the paragraph after (
A.91
) implies that

\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\left[\xi^{n}_{2,i}+\xi^{n}_{3,i}+\xi^{n}_{4,i}+\xi^{n}_{5,i}+\xi^{n}_{6,i}\right]\to 0.

We are left with

\begin{split}&\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\left[\xi^{n}_{1,i}-\frac{1}{k_{n}}\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\int K^{2}(u)du\right]\\
&=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\left[\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\frac{1}{k_{n}}\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\int K^{2}(u)du\right]\\
&=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\left[\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)\right.\\
&\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)\\
&\quad+\left.\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\Delta_{n}^{2}-\frac{1}{k_{n}}\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\int K^{2}(u)du\right]\\
&=:P_{1}+P_{2}+P_{3}.\end{split}

(A.108)

For
P_{1}
, note that

\displaystyle P_{1}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)\right]K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{11}+P_{12},

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|P_{11}\right|\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)\right]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{\left|t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right|}\Delta_{n}^{2}\leq C\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{b}}\int K^{2}(u)\sqrt{\left|u\right|}du\to 0.

Write
P_{12}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n}
, with

\zeta_{j}^{n}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Applying (
A.9
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{j}^{n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\int K^{2}(u)du\to 0.

Also,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta_{j}^{n}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{j}^{n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{j}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{j-1}\right)\right)^{2}\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right)^{2}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{n}^{3}}{b^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\int K^{2}(u)du\right)^{2}\to 0.

Thus,
P_{12}\to 0
and hence, we have that
P_{1}
converges to 0. Applying Lemma
A.1
with
\tilde{K}=K^{2}
, then
\tilde{K}_{b}(x)=bK_{{b}}^{2}(x)
and

\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)-\frac{1}{b}\int K^{2}(u)du=O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right).

Thus,

\displaystyle P_{3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\left[\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\Delta_{n}^{2}-\frac{1}{k_{n}}\int K^{2}(u)du\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c^{n}_{i}\right)\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}O\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta^{3/2}_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right).

which implies that
P_{3}
converges to
0
. For
P_{2}
, we proceed as follows:

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{j-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}-\check{c}^{n,pq,nv}_{i}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)\right|\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{|t_{j-1}-t_{i}|}\\
&\leq C\frac{\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{b}}\int K^{2}(u)\sqrt{|u|}du.\end{split}

Therefore,
\mathbb{E}|P_{2}|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{\sqrt{b}}\leq C\frac{1}{\sqrt{k_{n}}}\to 0
.
This shows that
\bar{V}^{n,4}\to 0
and we conclude (
2.33
).

Appendix B
Other Technical Proofs

B.1
Technical Proofs related to Section
A.1

Proof of (
A.11
).

Suppose
q\geq 1
. Note that

\displaystyle\alpha_{j}^{n}

\displaystyle=\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}\right)\left(\Delta_{j}^{n}X^{\prime}\right)^{*}-c_{j-1}^{n}\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left[2\mu^{\prime}_{s}\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)+\left(c_{s}-c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right]ds+2\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)\sigma_{s}dW_{s}.

We check first the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{1}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}2\mu^{\prime}_{s}\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)ds\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.1)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{q-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}2\mu^{\prime}_{s}\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)ds\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{q-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\in\left[0,\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}+s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right\|^{q}\right]\Delta_{n}^{q}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{q-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Delta_{n}^{q/2}\leq C\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left|K(u)\right|du\right)^{q}\Delta_{n}^{q/2},

where the third inequality holds with (
A.7
) and the boundedness of
\mu^{\prime}_{s}
. Similarly, by BDG inequality, (
A.7
), and the boundedness of
\sigma_{s}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{2}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)\sigma_{s}dW_{s}\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.2)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}\leq s\leq t_{j}\right\}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)\sigma_{s}dW_{s}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}\leq s\leq t_{j}\right\}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)\sigma_{s}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}\leq s\leq t_{j}\right\}}\left\|2\left(X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right)\sigma_{s}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left\|X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\right)^{q/2-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\left\|X^{\prime}_{s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\right)^{q/2-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{s\in\left[0,\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}+s}-X^{\prime}_{t_{j-1}}\right\|^{q}\right]\Delta_{n}^{q/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\right)^{q/2}\Delta_{n}^{q}\leq C\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K(u)^{2}du\right)^{q/2}\Delta_{n}^{q/2}b^{-q/2}.

Then, by the boundedness of
\tilde{\mu}_{t}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{3}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{s}\tilde{\mu}_{t}dtds\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.3)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\right)^{q}\leq C\left(\int\left|K(u)\right|du\right)^{q}\Delta_{n}^{q}.

With the boundedness of
\tilde{\sigma}_{s}
, for some
s_{j}\in[t_{j-1},t_{j}]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{4}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{t_{j}}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{s}\tilde{\sigma}_{t}dW_{t}ds\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.4)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{j-1}}^{s_{j}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}dW_{s}\Delta_{n}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}\leq s\leq s_{j}\right\}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}\Delta_{n}dW_{s}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}\leq s\leq s_{j}\right\}}\Delta_{n}^{2}\left\|\tilde{\sigma}_{s}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)^{2}\Delta_{n}^{3}\right)^{q/2}\leq\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K(u)^{2}dub^{-1}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)^{q/2}\leq C_{q}\Delta_{n}^{q}b^{-q/2}.

Finally, since

c_{j-1}^{n}-c_{i}^{n}=\int^{t_{j-1}}_{t_{i}}\tilde{\mu}_{s}ds+\int^{t_{j-1}}_{t_{i}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}dW_{s},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{5}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int^{t_{j-1}}_{t_{i}}\tilde{\mu}_{s}ds\Delta_{n}\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.5)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\left|t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right|\right)^{q}\leq C\left(\int\left|K(u)u\right|du\right)^{q}b^{q}.

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|B_{6}\right\|^{q}\right]:=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\int_{t_{i}}^{t_{j-1}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}dW_{s}\Delta_{n}\right\|^{q}\right]

(B.6)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq s\leq t_{j-1}\right\}}\Delta_{n}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}dW_{s}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq s\leq t_{j-1}\right\}}\Delta_{n}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}\right\|^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{j,l}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq s\leq t_{j-1}\right\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq s\leq t_{l-1}\right\}}\Delta_{n}^{2}ds\right)^{q/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\sum_{j}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sum_{l,l\leq j}K_{b}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i}\right)\Delta_{n}^{2}\right)^{q/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K(u)du\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K(u)udub\right)^{q/2}\leq C_{q}b^{q/2}.

Recall the definition of
\beta_{i}^{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{q}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{\bar{K}(t_{i})}\sum_{j=1}^{6}B_{j}\right\|^{q}\right]\leq C_{q}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{j=1}^{6}\left\|B_{j}\right\|\right)^{q}\right].

Then, applying Multinomial theorem, the cross terms can be bounded by Cauchy-Schwartz inequality with (
B.1
)-(
B.6
). In conclusion, we obtain

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{q}\right]\leq C_{q}\left(\Delta_{n}^{q/2}b^{-q/2}+b^{q/2}\right),\quad\text{for}\ q\geq 1.

∎

Proof of (
A.12
).

From Lemma
A.1
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{i-1}-\tau\right)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}(s-\tau)ds+D_{1}(1)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\tau/{b}}^{(T-\tau)/{b}}K(u)du+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)

\displaystyle=1-\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-\tau/{b}}+\int_{(T-\tau)/{b}}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right).

Therefore, using the Assumption
2
-c(ii) and denoting
\underline{\tau}=\inf\{\tau\in K\}>0
and
\bar{\tau}=\sup\{\tau\in K\}<T
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in K}\left|\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{i-1}-\tau\right)-1\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-\underline{\tau}/{b}}+\int_{(T-\bar{\tau})/{b}}^{\infty}\right)|K(u)|du+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\frac{\underline{\tau}}{b}\right)^{-1-\varepsilon}+C\left(\frac{T-\bar{\tau}}{b}\right)^{-1-\varepsilon}+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right).

∎

Proof of (
A.13
).

To show
\left|\bar{K}\left(t\right)\right|=\left|\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t\right)\right|>\delta
uniformly on
[0,T]
for some
\delta>0
, note that by Lemma
A.1
,
\forall t
,

\bar{K}\left(t\right)=1-\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}+\int_{\left(T-t\right)/h}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du+D_{1}(1)

where

D_{1}(1)=\frac{1}{2}\left[K\left(A^{+}\right)-K\left(B^{-}\right)\right]\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+o\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)\to 0

uniformly. For any
\epsilon_{0}\in(0,T/2)
, when
t\in[\epsilon_{0},T-\epsilon_{0}]
,

\displaystyle\left|\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}+\int_{\left(T-t\right)/h}^{\infty}\right)K(u)du\right|\leq\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}+\int_{\left(T-t\right)/h}^{\infty}\right)\left|K(u)\right|du

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{-\infty}^{-\epsilon_{0}/h}+\int_{\epsilon_{0}/h}^{\infty}\right)\left|K(u)\right|du\to 0

uniformly. When
t\in[0,\epsilon_{0})
, note that

\left|\int_{\left(T-t\right)/h}^{\infty}K(u)du\right|\to 0
uniformly. If
t=0
,

\left|1-\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}K(u)du\right|=\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}K(u)du\right|>\delta.

If
t\neq 0
, consider the property when
\left|x\right|\to\infty
,
K(x)x^{2}\to 0
. Thus,
\exists x_{0}
, s.t.
\forall\left|x\right|>x_{0}
, we have
\left|K(x)\right|\leq\frac{1}{x^{2}}
. Then,
\forall t\in(0,\epsilon_{0})
,
\exists h
, s.t.
h<\frac{t}{x_{0}}
, and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}K(u)du\right|\leq\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}\left|K(u)\right|du\leq\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}\frac{1}{u^{2}}du=\frac{b}{t}\leq\frac{1}{x_{0}}.

Hence, we have

\left|1-\int_{-\infty}^{-t/h}K(u)du\right|>\delta,

where
\delta
is not related to
t
. The upper bound can be obtained by (
A.12
) and small enough
\delta
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
A.1
.

First, for finite number of undefined points of
K^{\prime}
in
(A,B)\subset\mathbb{R}
, the same arguments in
A.2.5
can be applied so that we may assume that
K^{\prime}
is defined in all
(A,B)\subset\mathbb{R}
. For
\tau\in(0,T)
, the term
D_{1}(f)
can be written as

\displaystyle D_{1}(f)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[K\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{t-\tau}{b}\right)\right]f(c_{t})dt

(B.7)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{t}-\tau}{b}\right)\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{t}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right]\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt,

\displaystyle=D_{11}+D_{12},

for some
s_{t}\in(t_{i-1},t_{i})
.
D_{12}
can be controlled as the following:

\displaystyle\left|D_{12}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M_{f}\Delta^{2}}{2b^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{t\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t-\tau}{b}\right)\right|

(B.8)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M_{f}\Delta^{2}}{2b^{2}}V_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)=O\left(\frac{\Delta^{2}}{b^{2}}\right),

where
M_{f}:=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|f(c_{t})|<\infty
by (
A.1
) and the assumption on
f
, and
V_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)
is the total variation of
K^{\prime}
. Next, consider the term
D_{11}
. For any
\delta\in(0,\min(T-\tau,\tau))
, we have

\displaystyle D_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b}\left(\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}+\sum_{\delta\leq\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt

\displaystyle\triangleq D_{111}+D_{112}.

For an arbitrary, but fixed,
\delta>0
, the term
D_{112}
is such that

\displaystyle\left|D_{112}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b}\sum_{\delta\leq\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{\left|t_{i-1}-t\right|}{b}|f(c_{t})|dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M_{f}\Delta}{2b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|\geq\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|=o\left(\frac{\Delta}{b}\right),

since, due to (c) and (d) of Assumption
2
, for some
s_{i-1}\in(t_{i-1},t_{i})
and for large enough
n
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|\geq\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|

(B.9)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|\geq\delta}\int_{\left(t_{i-1}-\tau\right)/b}^{\left(t_{i}-\tau\right)/b}\left|K^{\prime}(t)\right|dt+\frac{\Delta}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|\geq\delta}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{\delta/2b}^{+\infty}+\int_{-\infty}^{-\delta/b}\right)\left|K^{\prime}(t)\right|dt+\frac{\Delta}{b}V_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)=o(1).

Now consider the first term
D_{111}
. Fix
\varepsilon>0
. Since
c
and
f
are continuous, there exists
\delta=\delta(\varepsilon)>0
such that
\left|f(c_{t})-f(c_{s})\right|\leq\varepsilon
whenever
|t-s|<\delta
. Then,

\displaystyle D_{111}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{b}\left(\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta,K^{\prime}\leq 0}+\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta,K^{\prime}>0}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt

(B.10)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta,K^{\prime}\leq 0}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}(f(c_{\tau})+\varepsilon)dt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta,K^{\prime}>0}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}(f(c_{\tau})-\varepsilon)dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{f(c_{\tau})\Delta}{2b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon\Delta}{2b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|.

Similarly, a lower bound can be written as:

D_{111}\geq-\frac{f(c_{\tau})\Delta}{2b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-\frac{\varepsilon\Delta}{2b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|.

Note that, since
\int|K^{\prime}(x)|dx<\infty
,

\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|\leq\sum_{i}\frac{\Delta}{b}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|\longrightarrow\int|K^{\prime}(x)|dx.

Also, letting
s_{i-1}\in\left(t_{i-1},t_{i}\right)
be such that
\int_{\left(t_{i-1}-\tau\right)/b}^{\left(t_{i}-\tau\right)/b}K^{\prime}(x)dx=\frac{\Delta}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)
and, denoting

\delta^{+}:=\max\left\{t_{i}-\tau:t_{i}<\tau+\delta\right\}
and
\delta^{-}:=\min\{t_{i-1}-\tau:t_{i}>\tau-\delta\}
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\frac{\Delta}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)

(B.11)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\int_{\left(t_{i-1}-\tau\right)/b}^{\left(t_{i}-\tau\right)/b}K^{\prime}(x)dx+\frac{\Delta}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\left[K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\left(\delta^{-}/b,\delta^{+}/b\right)\cap(A,B)}K^{\prime}(x)dx+\frac{\Delta}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\left[K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=(K(B-)-K(A+))+o(1),

since

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{b}\sum_{\left|t_{i}-\tau\right|<\delta}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{s_{i-1}-\tau}{b}\right)\right|\leq\frac{\Delta}{b}V_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)=O\left(\frac{\Delta}{b}\right)=o(1),

\displaystyle\int_{\left(\delta^{-}/b,\delta^{+}/b\right)\cap(A,B)}K^{\prime}(x)dx\longrightarrow K(B-)-K(A+).

Combining the previous inequalities, we have

\displaystyle L-2\int|K^{\prime}(x)|dx\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\leq\liminf_{n\to\infty}\frac{b}{\Delta}D_{111}\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\frac{b}{\Delta}D_{111}\leq L+\int|K^{\prime}(x)|dx\frac{\varepsilon}{2}.

where
L:=(K(A+)-K(B-))f(c_{\tau})/2
. Since
\varepsilon
is arbitrary, we conclude that

D_{111}=\frac{(K(A+)-K(B-))f(c_{\tau})}{2}\frac{\Delta}{b}+o\left(\frac{\Delta}{b}\right)

and thus (
A.14
) is proved. Note that (
B.7
) and (
B.8
) still hold for
\tau=0
or
T
. When
\tau=0
,

\displaystyle\left|D_{11}\right|=\left|\frac{1}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}}{b}\right)\frac{t_{i-1}-t}{b}f(c_{t})dt\right|\leq\frac{M_{f}\Delta^{2}}{2b^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}}{b}\right)\right|,

where
\frac{\Delta}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{i-1}}{b}\right)\right|\to\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|K^{\prime}(s)\right|ds
. Thus,
\left|D_{11}\right|=O\left(\frac{\Delta}{b}\right)
. The same result can be proved for
\tau=T
. Then, we have
D_{1}(f)=O\left(\frac{\Delta}{b}\right)
when
\tau=0
or
T
.

For (
A.15
), first note that by (
A.1
) and (
A.7
), for
\tau\in[0,T]
,

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)f(c_{i}^{n})\Delta_{n}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}f(c_{s})ds\right|\\
&\quad\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)\right|\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left|f(c_{i}^{n})-f(c_{s})\right|ds\\
&\quad\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)\right|\Delta_{n}^{3/2}=O(\Delta_{n}^{1/2}).\end{split}

Therefore,

\begin{split}&D_{2}(f)=D_{1}(f)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)f(c_{t_{i}})\Delta_{n}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}(t_{i-1}-\tau)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}f(c_{s})ds\\
=&D_{1}(f)+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{1/2}\right).\end{split}

∎

B.2
Technical Proofs related to Theorem
2.1

Proof of (
A.41
).

Set

\displaystyle D_{n}:=\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\partial_{\ell m}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\Delta_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{t_{u+1}}^{T}\int_{0}^{t_{u}}K_{{b}}(s-t)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})dtds=o_{p}(1).

To this end, note that, by (
A.7
-iii) and the local boundedness of the second-order partial derivatives of
g
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|D_{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{u}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{i})-K_{{b}}(s-t)\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\right|dtds

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{u}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})\left(\partial_{\ell m}g(c^{n}_{i})-\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\right)\right|\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\left.\left|\partial_{\ell m}g(c_{t})\left(K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})-K_{{b}}(s-t)\right)\right|\right]dtds

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{u}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})\right|\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})-K_{{b}}(s-t)\right|\right]dtds

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{u}\left[\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})\right|\Delta_{n}^{2}+\Delta_{n}^{-1/2}\int_{t_{j}}^{t_{j+1}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left|K_{{b}}(t_{j-1}-t_{i})-K_{{b}}(s-t)\right|dtds\right]

\displaystyle=:P_{1}+P_{2}.

We have

P_{1}\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\int_{\frac{(t_{j-1}-t_{u})}{b}}^{\frac{t_{j-1}}{b}}|K(v)|dv\leq Cb\int_{0}^{\frac{(T-t_{u})}{b}}\int_{w}^{w+\frac{t_{u}}{b}}|K(v)|dvdw\leq Cb\int_{0}^{\infty}\bar{L}(w)dw\to 0,

where
\bar{L}(w)=\int_{w}^{\infty}|K(v)|dv\mathrm{1}_{w>0}
.
For
P_{2}
, note that, for some
\xi_{i}\in(t_{i-3},t_{i})
,

\begin{split}P_{2}\leq&C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=u+1}^{n-1}\sum_{i=1}^{u}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-\xi_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\\
\leq&C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=u}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|+\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=u}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{u-1}\left|K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-\xi_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\\
\leq&C\Delta_{n}^{1/2}b\sum_{j=u}^{n}\int_{(t_{j-1}-t_{u})/{b}}^{t_{j-1}/{b}}\left|K^{\prime}\left(v\right)\right|dv+\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=u}^{n}V_{-\infty}^{\infty}(K^{\prime})\\
\leq&C\frac{{b}^{2}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{w}^{\infty}\left|K^{\prime}\left(v\right)\right|dv+\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=u}^{n}V_{-\infty}^{\infty}(K^{\prime})\to 0.\end{split}

∎

Proof of (
A.65
).

Denote

\begin{array}[]{l}S_{1}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}-t_{i}\geq 0\right\}}\partial g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\Delta_{n},\\
S_{2}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}-t_{i}\geq 0\right\}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds,\\
S_{3}:=\int_{0}^{T}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-s\right)\bar{K}(s)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}-s\geq 0\right\}}\partial g(s)ds.\end{array}

(B.12)

Define

\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(x\right):=\frac{K(x)^{2}}{\bar{K}\left(t_{j-1}-bx\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{x\geq 0\right\}}=\frac{K(x)^{2}}{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K\left(x+\frac{t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}}{b}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{x\geq 0\right\}},

and differentiate it as

\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(x\right)=\frac{2K(x)K^{\prime}(x)\bar{K}\left(t_{j-1}-bx\right)-K(x)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{j-1}-bx\right)}{\left[\bar{K}\left(t_{j-1}-bx\right)\right]^{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{x\geq 0\right\}}.

Then, similar as the definition of
D_{1}(f)
in Lemma
A.1
,

\displaystyle D_{1}(\partial g)=S_{2}-S_{3}=\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\right]\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s_{t}}{b}\right)\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds=\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s_{t}}{b}\right)-\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds=:D_{11}+D_{12},

where
s_{t}\in\left(s,t_{i}\right)
. Then,

\begin{split}\left|D_{12}\right|&\leq\frac{M_{\partial g}\Delta_{n}^{2}}{2b^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)-\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\\
&\leq\frac{M_{\partial g}\Delta_{n}^{2}}{2b^{3}}\left\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\bar{K}(s)}{\bar{K}(s)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)}\right|\right.\\
&\left.+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}(s)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{2}-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)\bar{K}(s)^{2}}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{2}\bar{K}(s)^{2}}\right|\right\}.\end{split}

(B.13)

Note that

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\bar{K}(s)}{\bar{K}(s)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)}\right|\\
&\leq\frac{1}{\delta^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\left[K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)\right|\\
&\quad+\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\left[K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)-K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right]\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)\right|\\
&\quad+\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\left[\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right]\right|\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\left[V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}(K)+V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right|\right].\end{split}

(B.14)

Similarly, we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}(s)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{2}-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)\bar{K}(s)^{2}}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{2}\bar{K}(s)^{2}}\right|\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{4}}\left[V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\left(K^{2}\right)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)-\overline{K^{\prime}}(s)\right|+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}^{2}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}^{2}(s)\right|\right].\end{split}

(B.15)

Consider

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}\left(s_{i}\right)\right|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\bar{K}^{\prime}\left(s_{i}^{*}\right)\right|\left|t_{i}-s_{i}\right|,

where
s_{i}=\arg\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right|
and
s_{i}^{*}\in\left[s_{i},t_{i}\right]
. Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\bar{K}^{\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{l-1}-t}{b}\right)\left(-\frac{1}{b}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{l-1}-t\right)\Delta_{n}-\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(u-t\right)du\right]\left(-\frac{1}{b}\right)-\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\int_{0}^{T}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{u-t}{b}\right)du,

where
K^{\prime}_{b}(x):=\frac{1}{b}K^{\prime}\left(\frac{x}{b}\right)
. By Lemma
A.1
,
D_{1}(1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(t_{l-1}-t\right)\Delta_{n}-\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}K_{{b}}^{\prime}\left(u-t\right)du\right]=O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\right)
. Thus,

\bar{K}^{\prime}(t)=\frac{1}{b}\left[K\left(-\frac{t}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{T-t}{b}\right)\right]+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b^{2}}\right).

\forall\epsilon^{\prime}>0
,
\exists
positive constant
C\left(\epsilon^{\prime}\right)
such that
\forall x>C\left(\epsilon^{\prime}\right)
,
\left|K(x)\right|\left|x\right|^{2+\epsilon}<\epsilon^{\prime}
. Then, for
Ck_{n}+1\leq i\leq n-Ck_{n}
, since
\left|\frac{s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right|,\left|\frac{T-s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right|\geq\frac{Ck_{n}\Delta_{n}}{b}=C
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\bar{K}^{\prime}\left(s_{i}^{*}\right)\right|\left|t_{i}-s_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}+\sum_{i=1}^{Ck_{n}}+\sum_{i=n-Ck_{n}+1}^{n}\right)\frac{1}{b}\left[\left|K\left(-\frac{s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right)\right|+\left|K\left(\frac{T-s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right)\right|\right]\Delta_{n}+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}\frac{1}{b}\left[\left|\frac{s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right|^{-2}+\left|\frac{T-s_{i}^{*}}{b}\right|^{-2}\right]\Delta_{n}\epsilon^{\prime}+2Ck_{n}\frac{1}{b}2M_{K}\Delta_{n}+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2b\epsilon^{\prime}\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}t_{i-1}^{-2}+4CM_{K}+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b^{2}}\right),

where

\displaystyle\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}t_{i-1}^{-2}=\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left(t_{i-1}^{-2}-s^{-2}\right)ds+\int_{t_{Ck_{n}}}^{t_{n-Ck_{n}}}s^{-2}ds

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}2\Delta_{n}^{2}t_{i-1}^{-3}+\left(\frac{1}{t_{Ck_{n}}}-\frac{1}{t_{n-Ck_{n}}}\right)\leq 2C^{-3}\Delta_{n}^{-2}k_{n}^{-3}+C^{-1}\Delta_{n}^{-1}k_{n}^{-1}.

Hence,
b\epsilon^{\prime}\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=Ck_{n}+1}^{n-Ck_{n}}t_{i-1}^{-2}=O\left(\Delta_{n}^{-1}k_{n}^{-2}\right)+O(1)
. We conclude that
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right|<\infty
. Similar proof can be shown for
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)-\overline{K^{\prime}}(s)\right|
. Also note that

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}^{2}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}^{2}(s)\right|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\max_{s\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]}\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)+\bar{K}(s)\right|\left|\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)-\bar{K}(s)\right|<\infty.

By (
B.13
), (
B.14
) and (
B.15
), We can further conclude that
\left|D_{12}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{2}}{b^{3}}\right)
.

For any
\delta^{\prime}\in\left(0,\min\left(T-t_{j-1},t_{j-1}\right)\right)
,

\displaystyle D_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\left(\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}+\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\right)\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds

\displaystyle=:D_{111}+D_{112},

where

\displaystyle\left|D_{112}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\left|\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{\left|s-t_{i}\right|}{b}\left|\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)\right|ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M_{\partial g}\Delta_{n}}{2{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|\frac{2K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)}\right|+\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)}{\left[\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)\right]^{2}}\right|

Since for some
s_{i}\in\left[t_{i-1},t_{i}\right]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|\frac{2K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)}\right|+\left|\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\overline{K^{\prime}}\left(t_{i}\right)}{\left[\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)\right]^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\int_{\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)/h}^{\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i-1}\right)/h}\frac{2}{\delta}\left|K(t)K^{\prime}(t)\right|+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\left|K(t)^{2}\right|dt

\displaystyle+\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|\geq\delta^{\prime}}\frac{2}{\delta}\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s_{i}}{b}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}-K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-s_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{\delta/h}^{\infty}+\int_{-\infty}^{-\delta/h}\right)\frac{2}{\delta}\left|K(t)K^{\prime}(t)\right|+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\left|K(t)^{2}\right|dt+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left[V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}(K)+V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\left(K^{\prime}\right)+V_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\left(K^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=o(1),

we have
\left|D_{112}\right|=o_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right)
.

Fix
\varepsilon>0
. Since
c
and
\partial g
are continuous,
\exists\delta^{\prime}=\delta^{\prime}(\varepsilon)>0
such that
\left|\partial g\left(c_{t}\right)-\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)\right|<\varepsilon
,
\forall|t-s|<\delta^{\prime}
. Then,

\displaystyle D_{111}=\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}\\
\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\leq 0\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}\\
\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}>0\end{subarray}}\right)\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}\\
\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\leq 0\end{subarray}}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\left[\partial g\left(c_{t_{j-1}}\right)+\varepsilon\right]ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{{b}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}\\
\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}>0\end{subarray}}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{s-t_{i}}{b}\left[\partial g\left(c_{t_{j-1}}\right)-\varepsilon\right]ds

\displaystyle=-\frac{\partial g\left(c_{t_{j-1}}\right)\Delta_{n}}{2{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)+\frac{\varepsilon\Delta_{n}}{2{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|.

Similarly, we have

D_{111}\geq-\frac{\partial g\left(c_{t_{j-1}}\right)\Delta_{n}}{2{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)-\frac{\varepsilon\Delta_{n}}{2{b}^{2}}\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|.

Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{i:\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|<\delta^{\prime}}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|\partial\tilde{K}^{j,n}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{i}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)K^{\prime}\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\sum_{i}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\right|\rightarrow\frac{C}{\delta}\int\left|K(x)K^{\prime}(x)\right|dx+\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\int K(x)^{2}dx.

We get
D_{111}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right)
, and
D_{1}(\partial g)=S_{2}-S_{3}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right)
.

For
S_{1}-S_{2}
, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{j-1}-t_{i}\geq 0\right\}}\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\left(\partial g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)\right)ds\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\int_{t_{i-1}}^{t_{i}}\mathbb{E}\left|\partial g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial g\left(c_{s}\right)\right|ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}^{2}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Delta_{n}^{3/2}=\frac{C}{\delta}\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{b}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\left|K\left(\frac{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}{b}\right)^{2}\right|=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{b}\right).

Thus,
S_{1}-S_{3}=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{{b}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{1/2}}{b}\right)
.
∎

Proof of (
A.85
).

First note that for
i\geq l
,

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|+\left|\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|,

(B.16)

where
\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{l-1}^{n}\right)
. By Itô’s formula,

\begin{split}&\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}=\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left[2\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{t-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}+\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)\right]ds+2\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1},p}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r},\\
&\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)=\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\Big[\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\Big]ds+\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}.\end{split}

(B.17)

Thus, we have the following decomposition of the term
\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l}^{n}\right)\right]\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]
in (
B.16
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l}^{n}\right)\right]\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

(B.18)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{t-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,pq^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\frac{1}{2}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,pq^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}2\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle=:\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{1,1}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})+\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{1,2}(r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime},v^{\prime})+\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{1,3}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{1,4}(r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime},v^{\prime})+\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{2,1}(r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime})+\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{2,2}(r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{3,1}(r,p^{\prime},q^{\prime})+\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{3,2}(r,r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime},u^{\prime},v^{\prime})+\sum_{r=1}^{d}\sum_{r^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{p^{\prime},q^{\prime}=1}^{d}H_{4,1}(r,r^{\prime},p^{\prime},q^{\prime}),

which can be divided into four different groups of product of stochastic integrals, namely "
ds\cdot ds
" (
\left\{H_{1,j}\right\}_{j=1}^{4}
); "
ds\cdot dW_{s}
" (
\left\{H_{2,j}\right\}_{j=1}^{2}
); "
dW_{s}\cdot ds
" (
\left\{H_{3,j}\right\}_{j=1}^{2}
); and "
dW_{s}\cdot dW_{s}
" (
H_{4,1}
). For simplicity, we only show the upper bound of one term for each group.

Group 1 ("
ds\cdot ds
"): By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, the boundedness of
\mu_{s}^{\prime}
,
\tilde{\mu}_{s}
,
c_{s}
,
\tilde{c}_{s}
, and the local boundedness of third-order and fourth-order partial derivatives of
g
, together with (
A.7
),

\displaystyle\left|H_{11}\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

(B.19)

\displaystyle\leq C\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\sup_{t\in\left[0,\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}+t}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}}\right\|^{2}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}\left(t_{i}-t_{t-1}\right)\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right).

Similarly, we have

\left.\begin{array}[]{l}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,pq^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\\
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\\
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,pq^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\end{array}\right\}\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right).

Group 2 ("
ds\cdot dW_{s}
"): By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, the boundedness of
\mu_{s}^{\prime}
,
c_{s}
,
\tilde{c}_{s}
, and the local boundedness of third-order partial derivatives of
g
, together with (
A.7
),

\displaystyle\left|H_{21}\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\mu^{\prime}_{s,q}ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

(B.20)

\displaystyle\leq C\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\sup_{t\in\left[0,\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}+t}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}}\right\|^{2}\Delta_{n}^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{t-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\right]^{2}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sqrt{t_{i}-t_{l-1}},

where the BDG inequality is applied in the first inequality. Similarly,

\left|H_{22}\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{pq}\right)ds\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}dW_{s}^{r^{\prime}}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sqrt{t_{i}-t_{l-1}}.

Group 3 ("
dW_{s}\cdot ds
"):

\displaystyle\left|H_{31}\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

(B.21)

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\left(\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\left(\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|.

Note that for the first term,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1},p}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1},p}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|=0,

and by the mean value theorem, for some
\lambda\in[0,1]
, the second term

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\sum_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}=1}^{d}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}^{4}g\left(c_{s}+\lambda\left(c_{t_{l-1}}-c_{s}\right)\right)\left(c_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}-c_{t_{l-1}}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\sup_{t\in\left[0,\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}+t}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1}}\right\|^{2}\Delta_{n}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\sup_{t\in\left[0,(i-l+1)\Delta_{n}\right]}\left\|c_{t_{l-1}+t}-c_{t_{l-1}}\right\|^{2}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{a}\right]\right\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right),

where the last inequality holds with (
A.7
), (
A.3
), and (
A.10
). Meanwhile, with similar argument, we can show the third and fourth terms in the decomposition of (
B.21
) have the same upper bound. Thus,

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\mu}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right).

(B.22)

Similarly,
H_{32}
in Group 3 also satisfies

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial^{4}_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime},u^{\prime}v^{\prime}}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{u^{\prime}v^{\prime},r^{\prime}}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right).

Group 4 ("
dW_{s}\cdot dW_{s}
"): Since
H_{41}=0
when
r\neq r^{\prime}
, we only need to check for
r=r^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left|H_{41}\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}dW_{s}^{r}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

(B.23)

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\left(\sigma_{s}^{q,r}-\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}+\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)+\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}-\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}+\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}\right)ds\Bigg|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\Bigg]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\left(\sigma_{s}^{q,r}-\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\right)\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}-\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\left(\sigma_{s}^{q,r}-\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\right)\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\left(\sigma_{s}^{q,r}-\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\right)\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}-\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}-\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\left(\sigma_{s}^{q,r}-\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\right)\left[\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right]\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}-\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}\right)ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|.

The first term of the decomposition in (
B.23
) can be bounded by

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\left|\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]ds\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2},

with (
A.7
). By the mean value theorem, (
A.7
), (
A.3
), and (
A.10
), for the third term,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{t_{l-1}}^{q,r}\left(\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{t_{l-1}}\right)\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{l-1}}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}ds\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left\|c_{s}-c_{t_{l-1}}\right\|^{2}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right\}^{1/2}ds\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\eta^{n}_{l-1}.

Other terms in (
B.23
) can be controlled by the same upper bound similarly.
Thus,

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{l}}\left(X^{\prime}_{s,p}-X^{\prime}_{t_{l-1,p}}\right)\sigma_{s}^{q,r}dW_{s}^{r}\int_{t_{l-1}}^{t_{i}}\partial_{pq,uv,p^{\prime}q^{\prime}}^{3}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{p^{\prime}q^{\prime},r}dW_{s}^{r}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\eta^{n}_{l-1}.

(B.24)

Combining (
B.16
), (
B.19
), (
B.20
), (
B.22
), and (
B.24
), we conclude that

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right).

∎

Proof of (
A.89
).

We show that for
i^{\prime}\geq i\geq l
,

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l-1}\right).

(B.25)

Note that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)+\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)+\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|+C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left[\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{l-1}^{n}\right)\right]\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{l}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{l-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{l-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta_{l-1,i^{\prime}-l+1}^{n}\left(t_{i^{\prime}}-t_{l-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{l-1}^{n}\right),

where in the third inequality we apply (
A.9
), (
A.85
), Itô’s lemma and the arguments in the proof of (
A.85
).
∎

Proof of (
A.91
).

When
i,l-1<j
, by (
A.9
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|=\left|\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right).

When
i<j\leq l-1
, by (
A.9
) & (
A.85
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{l-1}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|+C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,l-j}^{n}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right).

Finally, when
i\geq j
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|,

where the second term can be bounded by
C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,i-j+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)
following the proof of (
A.85
) with the boundedness of
c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}
. The first term

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|=\left|\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|+C\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{l-1}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|,

where
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,i-j+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)
by (
A.85
). When
l\leq j
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{l-1}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|=\left|c_{l-1}^{n,pq}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right).

When
l>j
, by (
A.85
),

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}c_{l-1}^{n,pq}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,l-j}^{n}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right).

∎

B.3
Technical Proofs related to Theorem
2.2

B.4
Proof of (
A.105
).

Recall

\displaystyle\widehat{A}^{n,3}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\beta_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv},

and define

\displaystyle{A}^{n,3}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\beta_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv},

where
\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}=0\ \text{or}\ 1
. Note that for some
\xi=\hat{c}_{i}^{n}+\phi\left(c_{i}^{n}-\hat{c}_{i}^{n}\right)
, where
\phi\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{A}_{0,0}^{n,3}-A_{0,0}^{n,3}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\beta_{i}^{n}\right\|^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\xi)\right\|^{2}\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}-c_{i}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\Delta_{n}^{3/4}\to 0.

which also holds with
\widehat{A}_{1,0}^{n,3}
,
\widehat{A}_{0,1}^{n,3}
, and
\widehat{A}_{1,1}^{n,3}
as well. Then, in order to analyze the asymptotic behavior of
\widehat{A}^{n,3}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}
, we only need to consider
{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}
. To this end, we follow closely the analysis following (
A.56
). Indeed, one may check that

\displaystyle{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{r=1}^{6}\xi_{r,i}^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}

(B.26)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sum_{r=1}^{6}{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(r,p,q,u,v)=:\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sum_{r=1}^{6}{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(r),

where

\displaystyle\xi_{1,i}^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv},

\displaystyle\xi^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}_{2,i}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\right)\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\xi^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}_{3,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\alpha_{l}^{n,pq},

\displaystyle\xi^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}_{4,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{n-1}\sum_{j=l+1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\alpha_{l}^{n,pq},

\displaystyle\xi^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}_{5,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n},

\displaystyle\xi^{n,\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}_{6,i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

When
r=1
,

\displaystyle{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(1)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}

\displaystyle=:R+L.

Similar to (
A.58
), define

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}

and note that, similar to (
A.61
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}|L-M|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=j}^{n-2k_{n}+1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\sqrt{\left|t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right|\Delta_{n}^{4}}

(B.27)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\int_{-\infty}^{0}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})\sqrt{-ub}du\frac{1}{b}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}}\rightarrow 0.

Then, we aim to find the limit of

R+M=:\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv},

(B.28)

where
R+M
is an adapted triangular array with

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}^{n}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=j}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{j-1}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}

\displaystyle=:\zeta_{j}^{n,1}+\zeta_{j}^{n,2}.

By (
A.9
),

\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{j}^{n}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\mid{\mathcal{F}_{j-1}^{n}}\right]=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n}\left(\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2}+O_{p}\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\right),

(B.29)

which suggests to consider

A_{n}:=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2},\quad B_{n}:=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n,2}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\Delta_{n}^{2}.

(B.30)

First note that

\displaystyle\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\partial_{pq,uv}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}{\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)}\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}\wedge t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-s-\lambda_{\ell}b\right)}{\bar{K}(s+\lambda_{\ell}b)}ds+O\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b_{n}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b_{n}}\right),

which can be proved similarly as (
A.65
) in Appendix
B
. Therefore,

\displaystyle A_{n}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\int_{0}^{t_{j-1}\wedge t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-s-\lambda_{\ell}b\right)}{\bar{K}(s+\lambda_{\ell}b)}ds\Delta_{n}+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{\frac{t_{j-1}}{b}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K\left(u+\frac{t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}}{b}-\lambda_{\ell}\right)\right]^{-1}K(u-\lambda_{\ell})du\frac{1}{b}

\displaystyle+O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{b}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{s/b}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left[\int_{u-\frac{s}{b}}^{u+\frac{T-s}{b}}K(v-\lambda_{\ell})dv\right]^{-1}K(u-\lambda_{\ell})\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{u\geq\frac{s-t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}{b_{n}}\right\}}duds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int_{0}^{\infty}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})du,

where the second equality above can be shown similarly to (
A.67
).
For
B_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle B_{n}
