\displaystyle=\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{j-1}^{n}\right)\check{c}_{j-1}^{n,pq,uv}\int_{t_{j-1}}^{T\wedge t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}(s+\lambda_{\ell}b)^{-1}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-s-\lambda_{\ell}b\right)ds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}\int^{0}_{\frac{s-t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}{b}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left[\int_{u-\frac{s}{b}}^{u+\frac{T-s}{b_{n}}}K(v-\lambda_{\ell})dv\right]^{-1}K(u-\lambda_{\ell})duds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int^{0}_{-\infty}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})du.

Note that

\left|\zeta_{j}^{n}\right|\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\leq C\frac{1}{b_{n}\Delta_{n}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})du,

and, thus, the remainder term produced by
O(\Delta_{n}^{5/2})
in (
B.29
) will vanish because

\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}O\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left|\zeta_{j}^{n}\right|\leq\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}O\left(\Delta_{n}^{5/2}\right)\frac{1}{b_{n}\Delta_{n}^{2}}\leq O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b_{n}}\right)=o_{p}(1).

Also, due to (
A.8
),

\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{j}^{n}\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\leq C\Delta_{n}\left|\zeta_{j}^{n}\right|^{2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\alpha_{j}^{n}\right\|^{4}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]=O_{p}\left(\frac{\Delta_{n}^{5}}{b^{2}\Delta_{n}^{3}}\right)=o_{p}(1).

Then, we conclude that

{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(1)\to\frac{1}{8\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int^{\infty}_{-\infty}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})du.

For
r=2
, we proceed as in (
A.72
)-(
A.73
) when dealing with
S_{n,2}
. Specifically, with the notation
c_{\cdot}^{n,1}:=c_{\cdot}^{n,pq}
,
c_{\cdot}^{n,2}:=c_{\cdot}^{n,uv}
,
c_{\cdot}^{(1)}=c_{\cdot}^{pq}
, and
c_{\cdot}^{(2)}=c_{\cdot}^{uv}
,

\displaystyle{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(2)=\frac{\Delta_{n}^{5/2}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{j-1}^{n,\ell}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left[\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{(\ell)}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)ds+\frac{\Delta_{n}\Delta K}{2b}\left(c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}-1}^{n,\ell}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)\right]+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\left(\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{(\ell)}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)ds\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{16b}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)ds\left(c_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}-1}^{n,uv}-c_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta K

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\Delta_{n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}{16b}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g\left(c_{i}^{n}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{uv}-c_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}^{n,uv}\right)ds\left(c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta K+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=:Q+P_{1}+P_{2}+o_{p}(1).

(B.31)

As in (
A.74
), we can show that

\begin{split}P_{\ell}&=O\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{(\ell)}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)ds\right|\right).\end{split}

(B.32)

Now consider
Q
. First, by integration by parts and denoting
\tilde{\sigma}^{(1)}=\tilde{\sigma}^{pq}
and
\tilde{\sigma}^{(2)}=\tilde{\sigma}^{uv}
, note that:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}K_{b}\left(s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{s}^{(\ell)}-c_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}^{n,\ell}\right)ds

(B.33)

\displaystyle=-L\left(\frac{T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{{b}}\right)\left(c_{T}^{(\ell)}-c_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}^{(\ell)}\right)+L\left(\frac{-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{{b}}\right)\left(c_{0}^{(\ell)}-c_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}^{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{{b}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell)}_{s}dW_{s}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle=:R^{(\ell)}_{1,i}+R^{(\ell)}_{2,i}+\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell)}_{s}dW_{s}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}}).

Then, as in Eq. (
A.76
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}R_{1,i}^{(\ell)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}|L\left(\frac{T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)|\left(T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\frac{b^{2}}{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}|L(u-\lambda_{\ell})||u-\lambda_{\ell}|du\xrightarrow{n\rightarrow\infty}0.

In the same way,

\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}R_{2,i}^{\ell}\right|\xrightarrow{n\rightarrow\infty}0.

To analyze the term coming from multiplying the
dW
terms in (
B.33
), we consider two cases. For
t_{i}<\sqrt{b}
, by Cauchy’s and BDG’s inequalities,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg|\sum_{i=1,t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{s-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{{b}}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell)}_{s}dW_{s}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}b\sum_{t_{i}<\sqrt{b}}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\int L^{2}(u)du\leq\frac{C}{\delta}\sqrt{b}\rightarrow 0.

For
t_{i}>\sqrt{b}
, by the proof of Theorem 6.2 in
Figueroa-López & Li (2020)
,
\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell)}_{t}dW_{t}=\sum_{r=1}^{d}\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell),r}_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}-\sqrt{b}}\int_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)dW^{r}_{t}+o_{p}({b}^{\frac{1}{2}})
. Since
b\ll\sqrt{b}
,
t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}=t_{i}+\lambda_{\ell}b
, and
b\to{}0
, we can replace
\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell),r}_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}-\sqrt{b}}
and
\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})
with
\tilde{\sigma}^{(\ell),r}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}
and
\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}})
, respectively. Thus, for any
r_{1},r_{2}\in\{1,\dots,d\}
, it suffices to consider the asymptotic behavior of

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{(\ell),r_{\ell}}\int_{t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)dW^{r_{\ell}}_{t}=:\sum_{i=1,t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta_{i}^{n}.

When
r_{1}=r_{2}=:r
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1,t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{i}^{n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]=\sum_{i=1,t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\tilde{\sigma}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}^{(\ell),r}

\displaystyle\times\int_{t_{i}+(\lambda_{1}\vee\lambda_{2})k_{n}-\sqrt{b}}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}}{b}\right)L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}}{b}\right)dt

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{n-2k_{n}+1}-\sqrt{b}}\frac{\theta}{8}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left(\int_{-\frac{s+\sqrt{b}}{b}}^{\frac{T-s-\sqrt{b}}{b}}K(v-\lambda_{\ell})dv\right)^{-1}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{(\ell),r}\int_{(\lambda_{1}\vee\lambda_{2})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{b}}}^{\frac{T-s-\sqrt{b}}{b}}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}L(u-\lambda_{\ell})duds+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{pq,r}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{uv,r}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u-\lambda_{1})L(u-\lambda_{2})du\right).

When
r_{1}\neq r_{2}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{i}^{n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]=0
.
By Cauchy’s and BDG’s inequalities, we also have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1,t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta_{i}^{n}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{i}^{n}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{i}-\sqrt{b}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\Delta_{n}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L^{2}\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)du\right)\xrightarrow{n\rightarrow\infty}0

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{t_{i}>\sqrt{b}}\Delta_{n}\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L^{2}(u-\lambda_{\ell})dub^{2}\xrightarrow{n\rightarrow\infty}0.

We then conclude the following asymptotics for the term
Q
in (
B.31
):

Q\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}\frac{\theta}{8}\sum_{r=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{pq,r}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{uv,r}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u-\lambda_{1})L(u-\lambda_{2})du\right).

(B.34)

As for
P_{\ell}
, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}R_{1,i}^{(\ell)}\right|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left|L\left(\frac{T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\left(c_{T}^{(\ell)}-c_{t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}}^{(\ell)}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\left|L\left(\frac{T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\right|\left(T-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{1/2}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|L(u)|b\sqrt{bu}du

\displaystyle=b^{3/2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|L(u)|\sqrt{u}du\rightarrow 0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t}^{(\ell)}dW_{t}\right|\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{t}^{(\ell)}dW_{t}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\int_{0}^{T}L\left(\frac{t-t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}}{b}\right)^{2}dt}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u)^{2}dub}\rightarrow 0.

Then, using (
B.32
) and (
B.33
), we conclude that
P_{\ell}\to 0
. Thus, as
n\rightarrow\infty
,

{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(2)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}\frac{\theta}{8}\sum_{r=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{pq,r}\tilde{\sigma}_{s}^{uv,r}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u-\lambda_{1})L(u-\lambda_{2})du\right).

For
r=3
, we split the inner summation into three parts:

\begin{split}{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(3)&=\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\\
&\qquad\times\left(\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}+\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{l=j+1}^{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{l=i+1}^{n}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\\
&=:R+L+Cross.\end{split}

Rewrite

\begin{split}R&=\sum_{l=3}^{n}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{\ell}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\times K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\\
&=:\sum_{l=3}^{n}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}.\end{split}

Then,

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}\right]\right|\leq C\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[|\zeta^{n}_{l}|\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)\right]\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{(j-1)\wedge(n-2k_{n}+1)}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)^{2}}\\
&\leq\frac{C}{\delta^{2}}\Delta_{n}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left(\eta^{n}_{l-1}\right)^{2}}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}|K(x)|dx\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0,\end{split}

and

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}\zeta^{n}_{l}\right)^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{l-1}\right]\right|\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\left(\zeta^{n}_{l}\right)^{2}]\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{(j-1)\wedge(n-2k_{n}+1)}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)|\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\right)^{2}\right]\\
&\quad+C\Delta_{n}^{2}\sum_{l=3}^{n}\left(\sum_{j=2}^{l-1}\sum_{i=1}^{(j-1)\wedge(n-2k_{n}+1)}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)|\right)^{2}\max_{j,j^{\prime}:j\neq j^{\prime}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left(\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,uv}_{j^{\prime}}\right)\right|\\
&\leq C\Delta_{n}b\sum_{l=3}^{n}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left|K(u)K(v+u)\right|du\right)^{2}dv+C\sum_{l=3}^{n}\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left|K(u)K(v+u)\right|dudv\right)^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\to\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.\end{split}

Hence,
R\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}}0
.
Similarly,
L
can be written as

\begin{split}L&=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=j+1}^{n}\sum_{i=l}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\alpha^{n,pq}_{l}=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n}_{j}.\end{split}

Then, similarly to (
A.86
), (
A.87
) and (
A.88
), we can apply (
A.85
) and (
A.89
) to show that

\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|\to 0,\quad\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\to 0.

(B.35)

The Cross term can be analyzed similarly to the case of
L
. We then conclude

{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(r)\to_{p}0,\quad\text{for}\ r=3\ \text{and}\ r=4.

(B.36)

When
r=5
, first note that by (
A.91
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|

(B.37)

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}<j,\\
C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{j-1,i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}-j+1}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i<j\leq i+\lambda_{1}k_{n},\\
C\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}-j+1}^{n}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right)+C\Delta_{n}\eta^{n}_{j-1,i-j+1}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)&\text{ if }i\geq j.\end{cases}

Then,

\displaystyle{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(5)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta_{j}^{n}.

By (
A.91
), (
B.37
), and the following decomposition:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|+\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|,

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha^{n,uv}_{j}\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g(c^{n}_{i})\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{j-1,\left|l-j\right|}^{n}\right]\left|t_{l-1}-t_{j-1}\right|+\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1,\left|i-j+1\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|+\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{j-1,\left|i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}-j+1\right|}^{n}\right]\left|t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right|\Big]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\right|\int_{-t_{i}/b}^{(T-t_{i})/b}\left|K(u-\lambda_{2})\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta^{n}_{j-1}\right]\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}+ub\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}+ub\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|}\right]\left|t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right|}\right]\left|t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right|\Big]du

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{(t_{j-1}-T)/b}^{t_{j-1}/b}\left|K(v-\lambda_{1})\right|\int_{v-\frac{t_{j-1}}{b}}^{v+\frac{T-t_{j-1}}{b}}\left|K(u-\lambda_{2})\right|\Big[\Delta_{n}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|u-v\right|b}\right]\left|u-v\right|b+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v\right|b}\right]\left|v\right|b+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v-\lambda_{1}\right|b}\right]\left|v-\lambda_{1}\right|b\Big]dudv

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}b\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int\left|K(v-\lambda_{1})\right|\int\left|K(u-\lambda_{2})\right|\Bigg[\frac{\Delta_{n}}{b}+\eta^{n}_{j-1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{b}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|u-v\right|b}\right]\left|u-v\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v\right|b}\right]\left|v\right|+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|v-\lambda_{1}\right|b}\right]\left|v-\lambda_{1}\right|\Bigg]dudv\to 0.

Meanwhile, by (
A.7
), (
A.8
), and Cauchy-Schwartz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{j}\mid\mathcal{F}^{n}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta^{n}_{j}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\bar{K}\left(t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{l=1}^{n}K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\left(c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\sum_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\times\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\left|c_{l-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right|\left|c_{l^{\prime}-1}^{n,pq}-c_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{5}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\sum_{l,l^{\prime}}\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right)\right|\left|K_{{b}}\left(t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{2}k_{n}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\times\left|t_{l-1}-t_{i+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right|^{1/4}\left|t_{l^{\prime}-1}-t_{i^{\prime}+\lambda_{1}k_{n}}\right|^{1/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\int\left|K(v-\lambda_{1})\right|\left|v-\lambda_{1}\right|^{1/4}dv\right)^{2}\left(\int\left|K(u-\lambda_{2})\right|du\right)^{2}\sqrt{b}\to 0.

Thus,
{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(5)\to_{p}0
. Following the same steps, we can show
{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}(r)\to_{p}0
when
r=6
. Thus,

\displaystyle{A}_{\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}}^{n,3}\to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\check{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\int^{\infty}_{-\infty}K(u-\lambda_{1})K(u-\lambda_{2})du

\displaystyle+\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial^{2}_{pq,uv}g\left(c_{s}\right)\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}L(u-\lambda_{1})L(u-\lambda_{2})du\right).

B.5
Proof of (
A.103
).

In what follows, we shall use the following estimate from
Jacod & Rosenbaum (2015)
(see Eq. (3.23) therein):

\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i\Delta_{n}+t}^{pq}-c_{i\Delta_{n}}^{pq}\right)\left(c_{i\Delta_{n}+t}^{uv}-c_{i\Delta_{n}}^{uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}^{n}\right]-t\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}\right|\leq Ct\eta_{i,k_{n}}^{n},

(B.38)

valid for any
t>0
, where

\eta_{i,k_{n}}^{n}=\max\left(\eta(Y)_{i,k_{n}}^{n}:Y=\mu,\tilde{\mu},c,\tilde{c},\hat{c}\right).

Recall that

\displaystyle\widehat{A}_{1}^{n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),

and define

\displaystyle A_{1}^{n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right).

Note that, by (
A.7
) and (
A.11
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{A}_{1}^{n,3}-A_{1}^{n,3}\right|

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg|\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\left(\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})-\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|c_{i+k_{n}}^{n}-c_{i}^{n}\right\|^{4}\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\xi)\right\|^{2}\left\|\hat{c}_{i}^{n}-c_{i}^{n}\right\|^{2}\right]\left(k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\left(k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right)^{3/2}\to 0.

Then, we only need to consider
A_{1}^{n,3}
, which can be written as

A_{1}^{n,3}=\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta^{n}_{i}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right).

By (
B.38
),

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}^{n}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\mathbbm{1}_{\left\{t_{i}\leq t_{n-2k_{n}+1}\right\}}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{k_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\int_{0}^{t_{n-2k_{n}+1}}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds+o_{p}(1)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{i}^{n}\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}^{n}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{i}^{n}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)^{2}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Delta_{n}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)^{4}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{4}\right]}\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}k_{n}\Delta_{n}^{2}\to 0.

Thus,

A_{1}^{n,3}\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds.

B.6
Proof of (
A.104
).

For simplicity, we only consider the term involving
\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)
. Consider

\begin{array}[]{l}\widehat{A}_{2}^{n,3}=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),\\
A_{2}^{n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),\end{array}

Again,
\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{A}_{2}^{n,3}-A_{2}^{n,3}\right|\to 0
, and using (
A.2
), we can write
A_{2}^{n,3}
as

\displaystyle A_{2}^{n,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left[\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)+\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}\right]

\displaystyle=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n,1}_{j}+\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta^{n,2}_{i},

where

\displaystyle\zeta^{n,1}_{j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),

\displaystyle\zeta^{n,2}_{i}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

\displaystyle=:\zeta^{n,21}_{i}+\zeta^{n,22}_{i}.

By (
A.91
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,1}_{j}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]\right|

(B.39)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{j-1}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\eta_{j-1}^{n}\right)+\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,\left|i+k_{n}-j+1\right|}^{n}\left|t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=j}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,i-j+1}^{n}\left(t_{i}-t_{j-1}\right)+\Delta_{n}\eta_{j-1,i+k_{n}-j+1}^{n}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j-1}\right)\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\Bigg(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\left(\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1}}\right]\right)\int^{t_{j-1}/b}_{0}\left|K(u)\right|du+b\int^{t_{j-1}/b}_{(t_{j-1}-T)/b)}\left|K(u)\right|\left|1-u\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},\left|1-u\right|b}\right]du

\displaystyle+b\int_{(t_{j-1}-T)/b}^{0}\left|K(u)u\right|\mathbb{E}\left[\eta_{t_{j-1},-ub}\right]du\Bigg)\to 0,

where in the second inequality we apply (
B.37
) with
\lambda_{1}=1
. By Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, (
A.7
), and (
A.8
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\right|\mathcal{F}_{j-1}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{j}^{n,1}\right)^{2}\right]

(B.40)

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\right)^{2}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\sum_{i,i^{\prime},i\neq i^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\right)^{2}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i^{\prime}}^{n})\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\left(c_{{i^{\prime}}+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i^{\prime}}^{n,uv}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\Delta_{n}^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{i,i^{\prime},i\neq i^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i^{\prime}}\right)\Delta_{n}^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta^{2}_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int K^{2}(u)du\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{b}+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\int K(u)du\right)^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}\to 0.

Thus,
\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n,1}_{j}\to 0
. Meanwhile, by (
A.7
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,21}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right|

(B.41)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\sum_{j=1}^{i}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\sqrt{t_{j-1}-t_{i}}k_{n}\Delta_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Ck_{n}\sqrt{\Delta_{n}b}\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\int\left|K(u)\right|\sqrt{\left|u\right|}du\to 0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,21}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{i}^{n,21}\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,21}\right)^{2}\right]

(B.42)

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)^{2}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}\leq i,j\neq j^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j^{\prime}-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j^{\prime}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta^{3}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{i}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{\left|t_{j}-t_{i}\right|}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+C\Delta^{3}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}\leq i,j\neq j^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j^{\prime}-1}-t_{i}\right)\left|t_{j}-t_{i}\right|^{\frac{1}{4}}\left|t_{j^{\prime}}-t_{i}\right|^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta^{2}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\int K^{2}\left(u\right)\sqrt{\left|u\right|}du\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{b}}+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\int K(u)\left|u\right|^{\frac{1}{4}}du\right)^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}b}\to 0,

which implies
\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta^{n,21}_{i}\to 0
. Now consider

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,22}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}

(B.43)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

(B.44)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]+\epsilon_{11}&\text{if}\ i<j\leq i+k_{n},\\
\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]+\epsilon_{12}&\text{if}\ j>i+k_{n},\end{cases}

where
\left|\epsilon_{11}\right|\leq C\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})
,
\left|\epsilon_{12}\right|\leq C\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}(t_{j}-t_{i+k_{n}})
. The proof of (
B.44
) can be found in Section
B.7.1
. Also, by (3.23) in
Jacod & Rosenbaum (2015)
,

\begin{array}[]{l}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}(j-i)\Delta_{n}\right|\leq C(j-i)\Delta_{n}\eta_{i,k_{n}}^{n},\\
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]-\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right|\leq Ck_{n}\Delta_{n}\eta_{i,k_{n}}^{n}.\end{array}

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,22}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

(B.45)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}(t_{j}-t_{i})+\epsilon_{11}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=i+k_{n}+1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}k_{n}\Delta_{n}+\epsilon_{12}\right)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}\int_{0}^{1}K(u)ubdu

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}b\int_{1}^{\infty}K(u)du+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}K(u)du+\int_{0}^{1}K(u)udu\right),

where
\epsilon_{11},\epsilon_{12}
are negligible as
o_{p}(1)
since

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\right.

(B.46)

\displaystyle\left.\left(\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\epsilon_{11}+\sum_{j=i+k_{n}+1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\epsilon_{12}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{3/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\Bigg(\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=i+k_{n}+1}^{n}\left|K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\right|\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}(t_{j}-t_{i+k_{n}})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}^{1/2}b^{3/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\int_{0}^{1}\left|K(u)\right|\sqrt{u}(1-u)du+\int_{1}^{\infty}\left|K(u)\right|(u-1)du\right)\to 0.

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,22}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{i}^{n,22}\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,22}\right)^{2}\right]

(B.47)

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)^{2}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}>i,j\neq j^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j^{\prime}-1}-t_{i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j^{\prime}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)^{2}\right]\Delta_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta^{3}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}K^{2}_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle+C\Delta^{3}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j,j^{\prime}>i,j\neq j^{\prime}}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i}\right)K_{b}\left(t_{j^{\prime}-1}-t_{i}\right)\left(t_{j}-t_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(t_{j^{\prime}}-t_{i}\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta^{2}_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\int K^{2}\left(u\right)\sqrt{\left|u\right|}du\frac{\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}}{\sqrt{b}}+C\Delta_{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\int K(u)\left|u\right|^{\frac{1}{4}}du\right)^{2}\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}b}\to 0.

Thus, we conclude that

A_{2}^{n,3}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{1}^{\infty}K(u)du+\int_{0}^{1}K(u)udu\right).

(B.48)

The convergence of
\widehat{A}_{3}^{n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i}^{n,uv}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)
can be shown in the same way.

B.7
Proof of (
A.105
).

We only need to consider

\begin{array}[]{l}\widehat{A}_{4}^{n,3}=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(\hat{c}_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),\\
A_{4}^{n,3}:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\beta_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right),\\
\end{array}

Again,
\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{A}_{4}^{n,3}-A_{4}^{n,3}\right|\to 0
, and, by (
A.2
),

\displaystyle A_{4}^{n,3}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left[\alpha_{j}^{n,pq}\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)+\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}\right]

\displaystyle=:\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n,1}_{j}+\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta^{n,2}_{i},

where
\sum_{j=1}^{n}\zeta^{n,1}_{j}\to 0
can be shown similarly as in (
B.39
) and (
B.40
). We decompose
\zeta^{n,2}_{i}
as
\zeta^{n,2}_{i}=\zeta^{n,21}_{i}+\zeta^{n,22}_{i}
,
where

\displaystyle\zeta^{n,21}_{i}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n},

\displaystyle\zeta^{n,22}_{i}

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{i}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\Delta_{n}.

We can show that

\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\zeta^{n,21}_{i}\to 0
in a similarly as in (
B.41
) and (
B.42
). Consider

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,22}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

(B.49)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\sum_{j=1}^{i}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\sum_{j=i+1}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

(B.50)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]+\epsilon_{21}&\text{if}\ i<j<i+k_{n},\\
\epsilon_{22}&\text{if}\ j\geq i+k_{n},\end{cases}

where
\left|\epsilon_{21}\right|\leq C\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})
,
\left|\epsilon_{22}\right|\leq C\sqrt{k_{n}\Delta_{n}}(t_{j}-t_{i+k_{n}})
. The proof of (
B.50
) can be found in Section
B.7.1
. By (3.23) in
Jacod & Rosenbaum (2015)
,

\begin{array}[]{l}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]+\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right|\leq Ck_{n}\Delta_{n}\eta_{i,k_{n}}^{n},\\
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j}\right]+\tilde{c}_{j}^{n,pq,uv}(i+k_{n}-j)\Delta_{n}\right|\leq C(i+k_{n}-j)\Delta_{n}\eta_{j,k_{n}}^{n}.\end{array}

Thus,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\zeta^{n,22}_{i}\left.\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{i}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\left(-\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}k_{n}\Delta_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=i+1}^{i+k_{n}-1}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\left(-\tilde{c}_{j}^{n,pq,uv}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})+\epsilon_{21}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{n}\sum_{j=i+k_{n}}^{n}K_{b}\left(t_{j-1}-t_{i+k_{n}}\right)\epsilon_{22}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\left(-\tilde{c}_{i}^{n,pq,uv}b\right)\int_{-t_{i}/b}^{0}K(u-1)du

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\Delta_{n}}}{8}\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{i}^{n})\bar{K}\left(t_{i+k_{n}}\right)^{-1}\int_{0}^{1}K(u-1)\tilde{c}_{t_{i}+ub}^{pq,uv}(u-1)bdu+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\to\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{0}^{1}K(u-1)(u-1)du-\int_{-\infty}^{0}K(u-1)du\right),

where
\epsilon_{21},\epsilon_{22}
are negligible similar as in (
B.46
). Also,
\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n-2k_{n}+1}\left(\zeta_{i}^{n,22}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\zeta_{i}^{n,22}\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right)\right)^{2}\to 0
can be shown similarly as in (
B.47
). Thus, we conclude that

A_{4}^{n,3}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle n\rightarrow\infty}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\theta}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sum_{p,q,u,v}\partial_{pq,uv}^{2}g(c_{s})\tilde{c}_{s}^{pq,uv}ds\left(\int_{0}^{1}K(u-1)(u-1)du-\int_{-\infty}^{0}K(u-1)du\right).

(B.51)

The convergence of
\widehat{A}_{5}^{n,3}
can be shown in the same way.

B.7.1
Proof of (
B.44
) & (
B.50
)

We proceed to show that when
i<j\leq i+k_{n}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]+\epsilon_{11},

where
\left|\epsilon_{11}\right|\leq C\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})
. Other cases can be proved similarly. Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{j}^{n,uv}-c_{i}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right],

where by (
A.7
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right|=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j}\right]\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left|c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(c_{i+k_{n}}^{n,uv}-c_{j}^{n,uv}\right)\right|\mathcal{F}_{j}\right]\right|\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right]\leq C\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left|c_{j}^{n,pq}-c_{i}^{n,pq}\right|\right|\mathcal{F}_{i}\right](t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j})

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{t_{j}-t_{i}}(t_{i+k_{n}}-t_{j}).
